syntax N = nat

syntax M = nat

syntax n = nat

syntax m = nat

syntax i = nat

syntax j = nat

def $min_(nat, nat) : nat =

  clause 0 : (i, j) = i
  -- if (i <= j)

  clause -1 : (i, j) = j

def $lsl_(int, int) : int =

  clause 0 : (int_1, int_2) = $lsl_((int_1 * 2 as int), (int_2 - 1 as int))
  -- if (int_2 > 0 as int)

  clause -1 : (int_1, int_2) = int_1

def $rev_<X>(X*) : X* =

  clause 0 : ([]) = []

  clause 1 : (X_h :: X_t*{X_t <- X_t*}) = $rev_<X>(X_t*{X_t <- X_t*}) ++ [X_h]

def $make_<X>(X, nat) : X* =

  clause 0 : (X, n) = []
  -- if (n = 0)

  clause 1 : (X, n) = $make_<X>(X, n') ++ [X]
  -- if (n > 0)
  -- if (n' as int = (n - 1))

def $drop_<X>(X*, nat) : X* =

  clause 0 : (X*{X <- X*}, n) = X*{X <- X*}
  -- if (n = 0)

  clause 1 : ([], n) = []
  -- if (n > 0)

  clause 2 : (X_h :: X_t*{X_t <- X_t*}, n) = $drop_<X>(X_t*{X_t <- X_t*}, n')
  -- if (n > 0)
  -- if (n' as int = (n - 1))

def $take_<X>(X*, nat) : X* =

  clause 0 : (X*{X <- X*}, n) = []
  -- if (n = 0)

  clause 1 : ([], n) = []
  -- if (n > 0)

  clause 2 : (X_h :: X_t*{X_t <- X_t*}, n) = X_h :: $take_<X>(X_t*{X_t <- X_t*}, n')
  -- if (n > 0)
  -- if (n' as int = (n - 1))

def $replace_slice_<X>(X*, nat, X*) : X* =

  clause 0 : (X*{X <- X*}, n, X_r*{X_r <- X_r*}) = X_r*{X_r <- X_r*} ++ $drop_<X>(X*{X <- X*}, |X_r*{X_r <- X_r*}|)
  -- if (n = 0)

  clause 1 : (X_h :: X_t*{X_t <- X_t*}, n, X_r*{X_r <- X_r*}) = X_h :: $replace_slice_<X>(X_t*{X_t <- X_t*}, n', X_r*{X_r <- X_r*})
  -- if (n > 0)
  -- if (n' as int = (n - 1))

def $list_exists<X>($p(X) : bool, X*) : bool =

  clause 0 : ($p, []) = false

  clause 1 : ($p, X_h :: X_t*{X_t <- X_t*}) = true
  -- if $p(X_h)

  clause 2 : ($p, X_h :: X_t*{X_t <- X_t*}) = $list_exists<X>($p, X_t*{X_t <- X_t*})
  -- if ~$p(X_h)

syntax set<K> = 
   | `{ K* } (from set<K>) 

def $empty_set<K> : set<K> =

  clause 0 :  = `{ [] }

builtin def $union_set<K>(set<K>, set<K>) : set<K>

builtin def $diff_set<K>(set<K>, set<K>) : set<K>

def $in_set<K>(K, set<K>) : bool =

  clause 0 : (K, `{ K'*{K' <- K'*} }) = K <- K'*{K' <- K'*}

def $map_set<X, Y>($f(X) : Y, set<X>) : set<Y> =

  clause 0 : ($f, `{ [] }) = `{ [] }

  clause 1 : ($f, `{ X_h :: X_t*{X_t <- X_t*} }) = $union_set<Y>(`{ [$f(X_h)] }, $map_set<X, Y>($f, `{ X_t*{X_t <- X_t*} }))

def $min_i64 : i64 =

  clause 0 :  = -9223372036854775808 as i64

def $le_u(i64, i64) : bool =

  clause 0 : (i64_1, i64_2) = true
  -- if (i64_1' = (i64_1 + $min_i64))
  -- if (i64_2' = (i64_2 + $min_i64))
  -- if (i64_1' <= i64_2')

  clause -1 : (i64_1, i64_2) = false

def $lt_u(i64, i64) : bool =

  clause 0 : (i64_1, i64_2) = true
  -- if (i64_1' = (i64_1 + $min_i64))
  -- if (i64_2' = (i64_2 + $min_i64))
  -- if (i64_1' < i64_2')

  clause -1 : (i64_1, i64_2) = false

def $range(nat) : nat* =

  clause 0 : (0) = []

  clause 1 : (n) = $range(n') ++ [n']
  -- if (n > 0)
  -- if (n' as int = (n - 1))

syntax null = 
   | NoNull (from null) 
   | Null (from null)

   | Cons (from mut) 
   | Var (from mut)

   | Set (from init) 
   | Unset (from init)

   | Final (from final) 
   | NoFinal (from final) 

syntax limits = {MIN i64, MAX i64?}

syntax range = i64

   | StatX typeidx (from typevar) 
   | RecX nat (from typevar)

   | StatX typeidx (from typevarConcrete)

   | I64AT (from addrtype)

   | NoneHT (from heaptype) 
   | EqHT (from heaptype)

   | ArrayHT (from heaptype)

   | NoExternHT (from heaptype)

   | null heaptype (from reftype)

def $subst_rectype<X>(X, $s(typevar, X) : heaptype, rectype) : rectype =

  clause 0 : (X, $s, rect) = RecT st'*{st' <- st'*}
  -- if (RecT st*{st <- st*} = rect)
  -- (if (st' = $subst_subtype<X>(X, $s, st)))*{st <- st*, st' <- st'*}

def $subst_subtype<X>(X, $s(typevar, X) : heaptype, subtype) : subtype =

  clause 0 : (X, $s, st) = SubT fin ht'*{ht' <- ht'*} strt'
  -- if (SubT fin ht*{ht <- ht*} strt = st)
  -- (if (ht' = $subst_heaptype<X>(X, $s, ht)))*{ht <- ht*, ht' <- ht'*}
  -- if (strt' = $subst_strtype<X>(X, $s, strt))

def $subst_heaptype<X>(X, $s(typevar, X) : heaptype, heaptype) : heaptype =

  clause 0 : (X, $s, ht) = ht
  -- if (((((((((((((ht = AnyHT) \/ (ht = NoneHT)) \/ (ht = EqHT)) \/ (ht = I31HT)) \/ (ht = StructHT)) \/ (ht = ArrayHT)) \/ (ht = FuncHT)) \/ (ht = NoFuncHT)) \/ (ht = ExnHT)) \/ (ht = NoExnHT)) \/ (ht = ExternHT)) \/ (ht = NoExternHT)) \/ (ht = BotHT))

  clause 1 : (X, $s, ht) = $s(tv, X)
  -- if (ht = VarHT tv as heaptype)

  clause 2 : (X, $s, ht) = DefHT dt
  -- if (ht = DefHT dt)

def $subst_strtype<X>(X, $s(typevar, X) : heaptype, strtype) : strtype =

  clause 0 : (X, $s, strt) = DefStructT structtype'
  -- if (strt = DefStructT structtype)
  -- if (structtype' = $subst_structtype<X>(X, $s, structtype))

  clause 1 : (X, $s, strt) = DefArrayT arraytype'
  -- if (strt = DefArrayT arraytype)
  -- if (arraytype' = $subst_arraytype<X>(X, $s, arraytype))

  clause 2 : (X, $s, strt) = DefFuncT functype'
  -- if (strt = DefFuncT functype)
  -- if (functype' = $subst_functype<X>(X, $s, functype))

def $subst_structtype<X>(X, $s(typevar, X) : heaptype, structtype) : structtype =

  clause 0 : (X, $s, StructT yt*{yt <- yt*}) = StructT yt'*{yt' <- yt'*}
  -- (if (yt' = $subst_fieldtype<X>(X, $s, yt)))*{yt <- yt*, yt' <- yt'*}

def $subst_arraytype<X>(X, $s(typevar, X) : heaptype, arraytype) : arraytype =

  clause 0 : (X, $s, ArrayT yt) = ArrayT yt'
  -- if (yt' = $subst_fieldtype<X>(X, $s, yt))

def $subst_functype<X>(X, $s(typevar, X) : heaptype, functype) : functype =

  clause 0 : (X, $s, FuncT resulttype_1 resulttype_2) = FuncT resulttype_1' resulttype_2'
  -- if (resulttype_1' = $subst_resulttype<X>(X, $s, resulttype_1))
  -- if (resulttype_2' = $subst_resulttype<X>(X, $s, resulttype_2))

def $subst_resulttype<X>(X, $s(typevar, X) : heaptype, resulttype) : resulttype =

  clause 0 : (X, $s, t*{t <- t*}) = t'*{t' <- t'*}
  -- (if (t' = $subst_valtype<X>(X, $s, t)))*{t <- t*, t' <- t'*}

def $subst_valtype<X>(X, $s(typevar, X) : heaptype, valtype) : valtype =

  clause 0 : (X, $s, t) = NumT nt
  -- if (t = NumT nt)

  clause 1 : (X, $s, t) = VecT vt
  -- if (t = VecT vt)

  clause 2 : (X, $s, t) = RefT rt'
  -- if (t = RefT rt)
  -- if (rt' = $subst_reftype<X>(X, $s, rt))

  clause 3 : (X, $s, t) = BotT
  -- if (t = BotT)

def $subst_reftype<X>(X, $s(typevar, X) : heaptype, reftype) : reftype =

  clause 0 : (X, $s, null ht) = null ht'
  -- if (ht' = $subst_heaptype<X>(X, $s, ht))

def $subst_storagetype<X>(X, $s(typevar, X) : heaptype, storagetype) : storagetype =

  clause 0 : (X, $s, ValStorageT t) = ValStorageT t'
  -- if (t' = $subst_valtype<X>(X, $s, t))

  clause 1 : (X, $s, PackStorageT pt) = PackStorageT pt

def $subst_fieldtype<X>(X, $s(typevar, X) : heaptype, fieldtype) : fieldtype =

  clause 0 : (X, $s, FieldT mut zt) = FieldT mut zt'
  -- if (zt' = $subst_storagetype<X>(X, $s, zt))

def $subst_deftype<X>(X, $s(typevar, X) : heaptype, deftype) : deftype =

  clause 0 : (X, $s, DefT rect n) = DefT rect' n
  -- if (rect' = $subst_rectype<X>(X, $s, rect))

def $s_roll(typevar, (typeidx, typeidx)) : heaptype =

  clause 0 : (tv, (x, y)) = VarHT RecX x'' as heaptype
  -- if (tv = StatX x')
  -- if ((x <= x') /\ (x' < y))
  -- if (x'' as int = (x' - x))

  clause -1 : (tv, (x, y)) = VarHT tv as heaptype

def $s_unroll(typevar, rectype) : heaptype =

  clause 0 : (tv, rect) = DefHT DefT rect n
  -- if (tv = RecX n)

  clause -1 : (tv, rect) = VarHT tv as heaptype

def $rollrt(typeidx, rectype) : rectype =

  clause 0 : (x, rect) = $subst_rectype<(nat, nat)>((x, y), $s_roll, rect)
  -- if (RecT st*{st <- st*} = rect)
  -- if (y = (|st*{st <- st*}| + x))

def $unrollrt(rectype) : rectype =

  clause 0 : (rect) = $subst_rectype<rectype>(rect, $s_unroll, rect)

def $rolldt(typeidx, rectype) : deftype* =

  clause 0 : (x, rect) = $rolldt'(0, rect')
  -- if (rect' = $rollrt(x, rect))

def $unrolldt(deftype) : subtype =

  clause 0 : (dt) = st*{st <- st*}[n]
  -- if (DefT rect n = dt)
  -- if (RecT st*{st <- st*} = $unrollrt(rect))

def $expanddt(deftype) : strtype =

  clause 0 : (dt) = strt
  -- if (SubT _fin _ht*{_ht <- _ht*} strt = $unrolldt(dt))

def $rolldt'(nat, rectype) : deftype* =

  clause 0 : (i, rect) = DefT rect i :: $rolldt'((i + 1), rect)
  -- if (RecT st*{st <- st*} = rect)
  -- if (i < |st*{st <- st*}|)

  clause -1 : (i, rect) = []

def $numtype_of_addrtype(addrtype) : numtype =

  clause 0 : (I32AT) = I32T

  clause 1 : (I64AT) = I64T

def $functype_of_strtype(strtype) : functype =

  clause 0 : (DefFuncT ft) = ft

def $min_addrtype(addrtype, addrtype) : addrtype =

  clause 0 : (at_1, at_2) = I64AT
  -- if ((at_1 = I64AT) /\ (at_2 = I64AT))

  clause -1 : (at_1, at_2) = I32AT

def $num_size(numtype) : int =

  clause 0 : (nt) = 4 as int
  -- if ((nt = I32T) \/ (nt = F32T))

  clause 1 : (nt) = 8 as int
  -- if ((nt = I64T) \/ (nt = F64T))

def $vec_size(vectype) : int =

  clause 0 : (V128T) = 16 as int

def $pack_size(packtype) : int =

  clause 0 : (I8) = 1 as int

  clause 1 : (I16) = 2 as int

  clause 2 : (I32) = 4 as int

  clause 3 : (I64) = 8 as int

def $unpacked_storage_type(storagetype) : valtype =

  clause 0 : (ValStorageT t) = t

  clause 1 : (PackStorageT pt) = NumT I32T

def $unpacked_field_type(fieldtype) : valtype =

  clause 0 : (FieldT mut zt) = $unpacked_storage_type(zt)

def $is_num_type(valtype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (NumT _nt) = true

  clause 1 : (BotT) = true

  clause -1 : (t) = false

def $is_vec_type(valtype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (VecT _vt) = true

  clause 1 : (BotT) = true

  clause -1 : (t) = false

def $is_packed_storage_type(storagetype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (ValStorageT _t) = false

  clause 1 : (PackStorageT _pt) = true

def $diff_reftype(reftype, reftype) : reftype =

  clause 0 : (null_1 ht_1, Null ht_2) = NoNull ht_1

  clause 1 : (null_1 ht_1, NoNull ht_2) = null_1 ht_1

def $defaultable(valtype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (NumT nt) = true

  clause 1 : (VecT vt) = true

  clause 2 : (RefT null ht) = (null = Null)

  clause 3 : (BotT) = false

syntax vecop<A> = 
   | V128 A (from vecop<A>) 

syntax laneop<A, B, C, D, E, F> = 
   | I8x16 A (from laneop<A, B, C, D, E, F>) 
   | I16x8 B (from laneop<A, B, C, D, E, F>) 
   | I32x4 C (from laneop<A, B, C, D, E, F>) 
   | I64x2 D (from laneop<A, B, C, D, E, F>) 
   | F32x4 E (from laneop<A, B, C, D, E, F>) 
   | F64x2 F (from laneop<A, B, C, D, E, F>) 

syntax op<A, B, C, D> = 
   | I32 A (from op<A, B, C, D>) 
   | I64 B (from op<A, B, C, D>) 
   | F32 C (from op<A, B, C, D>) 
   | F64 D (from op<A, B, C, D>) 

syntax memop<A, B> = {TYPE A, ALIGN int, OFFSET int, PACK B}

syntax vec_ = vecop<v128>

syntax instr = 
   | UNREACHABLE (from instr) 
   | NOP (from instr) 
   | DROP (from instr) 
   | SELECT valtype*? (from instr) 
   | BLOCK blocktype instr* (from instr) 
   | LOOP blocktype instr* (from instr) 
   | IF blocktype instr* ELSE instr* (from instr) 
   | BR labelidx (from instr) 
   | BR_IF labelidx (from instr) 
   | BR_TABLE labelidx* labelidx (from instr) 
   | BR_ON_NULL labelidx (from instr) 
   | BR_ON_NON_NULL labelidx (from instr) 
   | BR_ON_CAST labelidx reftype reftype (from instr) 
   | BR_ON_CAST_FAIL labelidx reftype reftype (from instr) 
   | CALL funcidx (from instr) 
   | CALL_REF funcidx (from instr) 
   | CALL_INDIRECT tableidx funcidx (from instr) 
   | RETURN (from instr) 
   | RETURN_CALL funcidx (from instr) 
   | RETURN_CALL_REF typeidx (from instr) 
   | RETURN_CALL_INDIRECT tableidx typeidx (from instr) 
   | THROW tagidx (from instr) 
   | THROW_REF (from instr) 
   | TRY_TABLE blocktype catch* instr* (from instr) 
   | LOCAL.GET localidx (from instr) 
   | LOCAL.SET localidx (from instr) 
   | LOCAL.TEE localidx (from instr) 
   | GLOBAL.GET globalidx (from instr) 
   | GLOBAL.SET globalidx (from instr) 
   | TABLE.GET tableidx (from instr) 
   | TABLE.SET tableidx (from instr) 
   | TABLE.SIZE tableidx (from instr) 
   | TABLE.GROW tableidx (from instr) 
   | TABLE.FILL tableidx (from instr) 
   | TABLE.COPY tableidx tableidx (from instr) 
   | TABLE.INIT tableidx elemidx (from instr) 
   | ELEM.DROP elemidx (from instr) 
   | LOAD memidx loadop_ (from instr) 
   | STORE memidx storeop_ (from instr) 
   | VEC.LOAD memidx vloadop_ (from instr) 
   | VEC.STORE memidx vstoreop_ (from instr) 
   | VEC.LOAD_LANE memidx vlaneop_ int (from instr) 
   | VEC.STORE_LANE memidx vlaneop_ int (from instr) 
   | MEMORY.SIZE memidx (from instr) 
   | MEMORY.GROW memidx (from instr) 
   | MEMORY.FILL memidx (from instr) 
   | MEMORY.COPY memidx memidx (from instr) 
   | MEMORY.INIT memidx dataidx (from instr) 
   | DATA.DROP dataidx (from instr) 
   | REF.NULL heaptype (from instr) 
   | REF.FUNC funcidx (from instr) 
   | REF.IS_NULL (from instr) 
   | REF.AS_NON_NULL (from instr) 
   | REF.TEST reftype (from instr) 
   | REF.CAST reftype (from instr) 
   | REF.EQ (from instr) 
   | REF.I31 (from instr) 
   | I31.GET extension (from instr) 
   | STRUCT.NEW typeidx initop_ (from instr) 
   | STRUCT.GET typeidx typeidx extension? (from instr) 
   | STRUCT.SET typeidx typeidx (from instr) 
   | ARRAY.NEW typeidx initop_ (from instr) 
   | ARRAY.NEW_FIXED typeidx u32 (from instr) 
   | ARRAY.NEW_ELEM typeidx elemidx (from instr) 
   | ARRAY.NEW_DATA typeidx dataidx (from instr) 
   | ARRAY.GET typeidx extension? (from instr) 
   | ARRAY.SET typeidx (from instr) 
   | ARRAY.LEN (from instr) 
   | ARRAY.COPY typeidx typeidx (from instr) 
   | ARRAY.FILL typeidx (from instr)

syntax const = instr*

def $type_num<A, B, C, D>(op<A, B, C, D>) : numtype =

  clause 0 : (I32 A) = I32T

  clause 1 : (I64 B) = I64T

  clause 2 : (F32 C) = F32T

  clause 3 : (F64 D) = F64T

def $type_vecop<A>(vecop<A>) : vectype =

  clause 0 : (V128 _A) = V128T

def $type_cvtop(cvtop_) : (numtype, numtype) =

  clause 0 : (I32 icvtop) = (I64T, I32T)
  -- if icvtop <- [WrapI64]

  clause 1 : (I32 icvtop) = (F32T, I32T)
  -- if icvtop <- [TruncSF32, TruncUF32, TruncSatSF32, TruncSatUF32, ReinterpretFloat]

  clause 2 : (I32 icvtop) = (F64T, I32T)
  -- if icvtop <- [TruncSF64, TruncUF64, TruncSatSF64, TruncSatUF64]

  clause 3 : (I64 icvtop) = (I32T, I64T)
  -- if icvtop <- [ExtendSI32, ExtendUI32]

  clause 4 : (I64 icvtop) = (F32T, I64T)
  -- if icvtop <- [TruncSF32, TruncUF32, TruncSatSF32, TruncSatUF32]

  clause 5 : (I64 icvtop) = (F64T, I64T)
  -- if icvtop <- [TruncSF64, TruncUF64, TruncSatSF64, TruncSatUF64, ReinterpretFloat]

  clause 6 : (F32 fcvtop) = (I32T, F32T)
  -- if fcvtop <- [ConvertSI32, ConvertUI32, ReinterpretInt]

  clause 7 : (F32 fcvtop) = (I64T, F32T)
  -- if fcvtop <- [ConvertSI64, ConvertUI64]

  clause 8 : (F32 fcvtop) = (F64T, F32T)
  -- if fcvtop <- [DemoteF64]

  clause 9 : (F64 fcvtop) = (I32T, F64T)
  -- if fcvtop <- [ConvertSI32, ConvertUI32]

  clause 10 : (F64 fcvtop) = (I64T, F64T)
  -- if fcvtop <- [ConvertSI64, ConvertUI64, ReinterpretInt]

  clause 11 : (F64 fcvtop) = (F32T, F64T)
  -- if fcvtop <- [PromoteF32]

def $type_lane<A, B, C, D, E, F>(laneop<A, B, C, D, E, F>) : numtype =

  clause 0 : (I8x16 _A) = I32T

  clause 1 : (I16x8 _B) = I32T

  clause 2 : (I32x4 _C) = I32T

  clause 3 : (I64x2 _D) = I64T

  clause 4 : (F32x4 _E) = F32T

  clause 5 : (F64x2 _F) = F64T

def $type_vec_lane<A, B, C, D, E, F>(vecop<laneop<A, B, C, D, E, F>>) : numtype =

  clause 0 : (V128 I8x16 A) = $type_lane<A, B, C, D, E, F>(I8x16 A)

  clause 1 : (V128 I16x8 B) = $type_lane<A, B, C, D, E, F>(I16x8 B)

  clause 2 : (V128 I32x4 C) = $type_lane<A, B, C, D, E, F>(I32x4 C)

  clause 3 : (V128 I64x2 D) = $type_lane<A, B, C, D, E, F>(I64x2 D)

  clause 4 : (V128 F32x4 E) = $type_lane<A, B, C, D, E, F>(F32x4 E)

  clause 5 : (V128 F64x2 F) = $type_lane<A, B, C, D, E, F>(F64x2 F)

def $lane_extractop(vecop<laneop<vnextractop<extension>, vnextractop<extension>, vnextractop<void>, vnextractop<void>, vnextractop<void>, vnextractop<void>>>) : int =

  clause 0 : (V128 I8x16 Extract (i, _extension)) = i as int

  clause 1 : (V128 I16x8 Extract (i, _extension)) = i as int

  clause 2 : (V128 I32x4 Extract (i, _void)) = i as int

  clause 3 : (V128 I64x2 Extract (i, _void)) = i as int

  clause 4 : (V128 F32x4 Extract (i, _void)) = i as int

  clause 5 : (V128 F64x2 Extract (i, _void)) = i as int

def $lane_replaceop(vecop<laneop<vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop>>) : int =

  clause 0 : (V128 I8x16 Replace i) = i as int

  clause 1 : (V128 I16x8 Replace i) = i as int

  clause 2 : (V128 I32x4 Replace i) = i as int

  clause 3 : (V128 I64x2 Replace i) = i as int

  clause 4 : (V128 F32x4 Replace i) = i as int

  clause 5 : (V128 F64x2 Replace i) = i as int

def $num_lanes<A, B, C, D, E, F>(vecop<laneop<A, B, C, D, E, F>>) : int =

  clause 0 : (V128 I8x16 _A) = 16 as int

  clause 1 : (V128 I16x8 _B) = 8 as int

  clause 2 : (V128 I32x4 _C) = 4 as int

  clause 3 : (V128 I64x2 _D) = 2 as int

  clause 4 : (V128 F32x4 _E) = 4 as int

  clause 5 : (V128 F64x2 _F) = 2 as int

def $type_externop(externop_) : (heaptype, heaptype) =

  clause 0 : (Internalize) = (ExternHT, AnyHT)

  clause 1 : (Externalize) = (AnyHT, ExternHT)

syntax type = rectype

syntax global = {GTYPE globaltype, GINIT const}

syntax local = {LTYPE valtype}

syntax table = {TTYPE tabletype, TINIT const}

syntax memory = {MTYPE memtype}

syntax tag = {TGTYPE typeidx}

syntax func = {FTYPE typeidx, LOCALS local*, BODY const}

syntax start = {SFUNC funcidx}

syntax active = {INDEX idx, OFFSET const}

syntax elemmode = 
   | Passive (from elemmode) 
   | Active active (from elemmode) 
   | Declarative (from elemmode) 

syntax elem = {ETYPE reftype, EINIT const*, EMODE elemmode}

syntax datamode = 
   | Passive (from datamode) 
   | Active active (from datamode) 

syntax data = {DINIT text, DMODE datamode}

syntax importdesc = 
   | FuncImport typeidx (from importdesc) 
   | TableImport tabletype (from importdesc) 
   | MemImport memtype (from importdesc) 
   | GlobalImport globaltype (from importdesc) 
   | TagImport typeidx (from importdesc) 

syntax exportdesc = 
   | FuncExport funcidx (from exportdesc) 
   | TableExport tableidx (from exportdesc) 
   | MemExport memidx (from exportdesc) 
   | GlobalExport globalidx (from exportdesc) 
   | TagExport tagidx (from exportdesc) 

syntax import = {MODULENAME name, ITEMNAME name, IDESC importdesc}

syntax export = {NAME name, EDESC exportdesc}

syntax module = {TYPES type*, GLOBALS global*, TABLES table*, MEMS memory*, TAGS tag*, FUNCS func*, START start?, ELEMS elem*, DATAS data*, IMPORTS import*, EXPORTS export*}

var ty : type

var loc : local

var glob : global

var tab : table

var im : import

var ex : export

syntax free = {TYPES set<typeidx>, GLOBALS set<globalidx>, TABLES set<tableidx>, MEMS set<memidx>, TAGS set<tagidx>, FUNCS set<funcidx>, ELEMS set<elemidx>, DATAS set<dataidx>, LOCALS set<localidx>, LABELS set<labelidx>}

def $free_empty : free =

  clause 0 :  = {TYPES $empty_set<typeidx>, GLOBALS $empty_set<globalidx>, TABLES $empty_set<tableidx>, MEMS $empty_set<memidx>, TAGS $empty_set<tagidx>, FUNCS $empty_set<funcidx>, ELEMS $empty_set<elemidx>, DATAS $empty_set<dataidx>, LOCALS $empty_set<localidx>, LABELS $empty_set<labelidx>}

def $fold_left<X, Y>($f(X, Y) : X, X, Y*) : X =

  clause 0 : ($f, X, []) = X

  clause 1 : ($f, X, Y_h :: Y_t*{Y_t <- Y_t*}) = $fold_left<X, Y>($f, $f(X, Y_h), Y_t*{Y_t <- Y_t*})

def $free_union(free, free) : free =

  clause 0 : (free_1, free_2) = {TYPES $union_set<typeidx>(free_1.TYPES, free_2.TYPES), GLOBALS $union_set<globalidx>(free_1.GLOBALS, free_2.GLOBALS), TABLES $union_set<tableidx>(free_1.TABLES, free_2.TABLES), MEMS $union_set<memidx>(free_1.MEMS, free_2.MEMS), TAGS $union_set<tagidx>(free_1.TAGS, free_2.TAGS), FUNCS $union_set<funcidx>(free_1.FUNCS, free_2.FUNCS), ELEMS $union_set<elemidx>(free_1.ELEMS, free_2.ELEMS), DATAS $union_set<dataidx>(free_1.DATAS, free_2.DATAS), LOCALS $union_set<localidx>(free_1.LOCALS, free_2.LOCALS), LABELS $union_set<labelidx>(free_1.LABELS, free_2.LABELS)}

def $free_unions(free*) : free =

  clause 0 : ([]) = $free_empty

  clause 1 : (free_h :: free_t*{free_t <- free_t*}) = free
  -- if (free' = $free_unions(free_t*{free_t <- free_t*}))
  -- if (free = $free_union(free_h, free'))

def $add_minus_one(idx) : idx =

  clause 0 : (x) = x'
  -- if (x' as int = (x - 1))

def $free_idx(idx) : set<idx> =

  clause 0 : (x) = `{ [x] }

def $free_shift(set<idx>) : set<idx> =

  clause 0 : (`{ x*{x <- x*} }) = `{ x''*{x'' <- x''*} }
  -- if (`{ x'*{x' <- x'*} } = $diff_set<idx>(`{ x*{x <- x*} }, `{ [0] }))
  -- if (`{ x''*{x'' <- x''*} } = $map_set<idx, idx>($add_minus_one, `{ x'*{x' <- x'*} }))

def $free_list<X>($free_(X) : free, X*) : free =

  clause 0 : ($free_, []) = $free_empty

  clause 1 : ($free_, X_h :: X_t*{X_t <- X_t*}) = $fold_left<free, free>($free_union, $free_(X_h), [$free_list<X>($free_, X_t*{X_t <- X_t*})])

def $free_opt<X>($free_(X) : free, X?) : free =

  clause 0 : ($free_, ?()) = $free_empty

  clause 1 : ($free_, ?(X)) = $free_(X)

def $free_catch(catch) : free =

  clause 0 : (Catch x y) = $free_union($update_tags($free_idx(x)), $update_labels($free_idx(y)))

  clause 1 : (CatchRef x y) = $free_union($update_tags($free_idx(x)), $update_labels($free_idx(y)))

  clause 2 : (CatchAll x) = $update_labels($free_idx(x))

  clause 3 : (CatchAllRef x) = $update_labels($free_idx(x))

def $free_type_(type) : free =

  clause 0 : (type) = $free_rectype(type)

def $free_global(global) : free =

  clause 0 : (global) = free
  -- if (free_1 = $free_globaltype(global.GTYPE))
  -- if (free_2 = $free_const(global.GINIT))
  -- if (free = $free_union(free_1, free_2))

def $free_table(table) : free =

  clause 0 : (table) = free
  -- if (free_1 = $free_tabletype(table.TTYPE))
  -- if (free_2 = $free_const(table.TINIT))
  -- if (free = $free_union(free_1, free_2))

def $free_mem(memory) : free =

  clause 0 : ({MTYPE mt}) = $free_memtype(mt)

def $free_func(func) : free =

  clause 0 : (func) = free[LOCALS = $empty_set<localidx>]
  -- if (free_1 = $update_types(`{ [func.FTYPE] }))
  -- if (free_2 = $free_block(func.BODY))
  -- if (free = $free_union(free_1, free_2))

def $free_start(start) : free =

  clause 0 : (start) = $update_funcs(`{ [start.SFUNC] })

def $free_elem(elem) : free =

  clause 0 : (elem) = free
  -- if (free_1 = $free_list<const>($free_const, elem.EINIT))
  -- if (free_2 = $free_elemmode($update_tables, elem.EMODE))
  -- if (free = $free_union(free_1, free_2))

def $free_data(data) : free =

  clause 0 : (data) = $free_datamode($update_mems, data.DMODE)

def $free_import(import) : free =

  clause 0 : (import) = $free_importdesc(import.IDESC)

def $free_export(export) : free =

  clause 0 : (export) = $free_exportdesc(export.EDESC)

def $free_rectype(rectype) : free =

  clause 0 : (RecT st*{st <- st*}) = $free_list<subtype>($free_subtype, st*{st <- st*})

def $free_subtype(subtype) : free =

  clause 0 : (SubT fin ht*{ht <- ht*} strt) = $free_union($free_list<heaptype>($free_heaptype, ht*{ht <- ht*}), $free_strtype(strt))

def $free_heaptype(heaptype) : free =

  clause 0 : (ht) = $free_typevar(tv)
  -- if (VarHT tv as heaptype = ht)

  clause -1 : (ht) = $free_empty

def $free_typevar(typevar) : free =

  clause 0 : (StatX x) = $update_types($free_idx(x))

  clause 1 : (RecX _nat) = $free_empty

def $free_strtype(strtype) : free =

  clause 0 : (DefStructT strctt) = $free_structtype(strctt)

  clause 1 : (DefArrayT arrt) = $free_arraytype(arrt)

  clause 2 : (DefFuncT ft) = $free_functype(ft)

def $free_structtype(structtype) : free =

  clause 0 : (StructT yt*{yt <- yt*}) = $free_list<fieldtype>($free_fieldtype, yt*{yt <- yt*})

def $free_arraytype(arraytype) : free =

  clause 0 : (ArrayT yt) = $free_fieldtype(yt)

def $free_functype(functype) : free =

  clause 0 : (FuncT t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}) = $free_union($free_list<valtype>($free_valtype, t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}), $free_list<valtype>($free_valtype, t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}))

def $free_fieldtype(fieldtype) : free =

  clause 0 : (FieldT _mut zt) = $free_storagetype(zt)

def $free_storagetype(storagetype) : free =

  clause 0 : (ValStorageT t) = $free_valtype(t)

  clause 1 : (PackStorageT pt) = $free_packtype(pt)

def $free_valtype(valtype) : free =

  clause 0 : (NumT nt) = $free_numtype(nt)

  clause 1 : (VecT vt) = $free_vectype(vt)

  clause 2 : (RefT rt) = $free_reftype(rt)

  clause 3 : (BotT) = $free_empty

def $free_packtype(packtype) : free =

  clause 0 : (pt) = $free_empty

def $free_numtype(numtype) : free =

  clause 0 : (nt) = $free_empty

def $free_vectype(vectype) : free =

  clause 0 : (vt) = $free_empty

def $free_reftype(reftype) : free =

  clause 0 : (_null ht) = $free_heaptype(ht)

def $free_globaltype(globaltype) : free =

  clause 0 : (GlobalT _mut t) = $free_valtype(t)

def $free_blocktype(blocktype) : free =

  clause 0 : (VarBlockType x) = $update_types($free_idx(x))

  clause 1 : (ValBlockType ?(t)) = $free_valtype(t)

  clause 2 : (ValBlockType ?()) = $free_empty

def $free_instr(instr) : free =

  clause 0 : (UNREACHABLE) = $free_empty

  clause 1 : (NOP) = $free_empty

  clause 2 : (DROP) = $free_empty

  clause 3 : (SELECT ?()) = $free_list<valtype>($free_valtype, [])

  clause 4 : (SELECT ?(t*{t <- t*})) = $free_list<valtype>($free_valtype, t*{t <- t*})

  clause 5 : (BLOCK blocktype instr*{instr <- instr*}) = $free_union($free_blocktype(blocktype), $free_block(instr*{instr <- instr*}))

  clause 6 : (LOOP blocktype instr*{instr <- instr*}) = $free_union($free_blocktype(blocktype), $free_block(instr*{instr <- instr*}))

  clause 7 : (IF blocktype instr_t*{instr_t <- instr_t*} ELSE instr_f*{instr_f <- instr_f*}) = $free_union($free_blocktype(blocktype), $free_union($free_block(instr_t*{instr_t <- instr_t*}), $free_block(instr_f*{instr_f <- instr_f*})))

  clause 8 : (BR x) = $update_labels($free_idx(x))

  clause 9 : (BR_IF x) = $update_labels($free_idx(x))

  clause 10 : (BR_TABLE x*{x <- x*} y) = $free_list<idx>($update_label, y :: x*{x <- x*})

  clause 11 : (BR_ON_NULL x) = $update_labels($free_idx(x))

  clause 12 : (BR_ON_NON_NULL x) = $update_labels($free_idx(x))

  clause 13 : (BR_ON_CAST x rt_1 rt_2) = $free_union($update_labels($free_idx(x)), $free_union($free_reftype(rt_1), $free_reftype(rt_2)))

  clause 14 : (BR_ON_CAST_FAIL x rt_1 rt_2) = $free_union($update_labels($free_idx(x)), $free_union($free_reftype(rt_1), $free_reftype(rt_2)))

  clause 15 : (CALL x) = $update_funcs($free_idx(x))

  clause 16 : (CALL_REF x) = $update_types($free_idx(x))

  clause 17 : (CALL_INDIRECT x y) = $free_union($update_tables($free_idx(x)), $update_types($free_idx(y)))

  clause 18 : (RETURN) = $free_empty

  clause 19 : (RETURN_CALL x) = $update_funcs($free_idx(x))

  clause 20 : (RETURN_CALL_REF x) = $update_types($free_idx(x))

  clause 21 : (RETURN_CALL_INDIRECT x y) = $free_union($update_tables($free_idx(x)), $update_types($free_idx(y)))

  clause 22 : (THROW x) = $update_tags($free_idx(x))

  clause 23 : (THROW_REF) = $free_empty

  clause 24 : (TRY_TABLE blocktype catch*{catch <- catch*} instr*{instr <- instr*}) = $free_union($free_blocktype(blocktype), $free_union($free_list<catch>($free_catch, catch*{catch <- catch*}), $free_block(instr*{instr <- instr*})))

  clause 25 : (LOCAL.GET x) = $update_locals($free_idx(x))

  clause 26 : (LOCAL.SET x) = $update_locals($free_idx(x))

  clause 27 : (LOCAL.TEE x) = $update_locals($free_idx(x))

  clause 28 : (GLOBAL.GET x) = $update_globals($free_idx(x))

  clause 29 : (GLOBAL.SET x) = $update_globals($free_idx(x))

  clause 30 : (TABLE.GET x) = $update_tables($free_idx(x))

  clause 31 : (TABLE.SET x) = $update_tables($free_idx(x))

  clause 32 : (TABLE.SIZE x) = $update_tables($free_idx(x))

  clause 33 : (TABLE.GROW x) = $update_tables($free_idx(x))

  clause 34 : (TABLE.FILL x) = $update_tables($free_idx(x))

  clause 35 : (TABLE.COPY x y) = $free_union($update_tables($free_idx(x)), $update_tables($free_idx(y)))

  clause 36 : (TABLE.INIT x y) = $free_union($update_tables($free_idx(x)), $update_elems($free_idx(y)))

  clause 37 : (ELEM.DROP x) = $update_elems($free_idx(x))

  clause 38 : (LOAD x _lop) = $update_mems($free_idx(x))

  clause 39 : (STORE x _sop) = $update_mems($free_idx(x))

  clause 40 : (VEC.LOAD x _vlop) = $update_mems($free_idx(x))

  clause 41 : (VEC.STORE x _vsop) = $update_mems($free_idx(x))

  clause 42 : (VEC.LOAD_LANE x _vlaneop _int) = $update_mems($free_idx(x))

  clause 43 : (VEC.STORE_LANE x _vlaneop _int) = $update_mems($free_idx(x))

  clause 44 : (MEMORY.SIZE x) = $update_mems($free_idx(x))

  clause 45 : (MEMORY.GROW x) = $update_mems($free_idx(x))

  clause 46 : (MEMORY.FILL x) = $update_mems($free_idx(x))

  clause 47 : (MEMORY.COPY x y) = $free_union($update_mems($free_idx(x)), $update_mems($free_idx(y)))

  clause 48 : (MEMORY.INIT x y) = $free_union($update_mems($free_idx(x)), $update_datas($free_idx(y)))

  clause 49 : (DATA.DROP x) = $update_datas($free_idx(x))

  clause 50 : (REF.NULL ht) = $free_heaptype(ht)

  clause 51 : (REF.FUNC x) = $update_funcs($free_idx(x))

  clause 52 : (REF.IS_NULL) = $free_empty

  clause 53 : (REF.AS_NON_NULL) = $free_empty

  clause 54 : (REF.TEST rt) = $free_reftype(rt)

  clause 55 : (REF.CAST rt) = $free_reftype(rt)

  clause 56 : (REF.EQ) = $free_empty

  clause 57 : (REF.I31) = $free_empty

  clause 58 : (I31.GET _extension) = $free_empty

  clause 59 : (STRUCT.NEW x _iop) = $update_types($free_idx(x))

  clause 60 : (STRUCT.GET x _ti _extension?{_extension <- _extension?}) = $update_types($free_idx(x))

  clause 61 : (STRUCT.SET x _ti) = $update_types($free_idx(x))

  clause 62 : (ARRAY.NEW x _iop) = $update_types($free_idx(x))

  clause 63 : (ARRAY.NEW_FIXED x _u32) = $update_types($free_idx(x))

  clause 64 : (ARRAY.NEW_ELEM x y) = $free_union($update_types($free_idx(x)), $update_elems($free_idx(y)))

  clause 65 : (ARRAY.NEW_DATA x y) = $free_union($update_types($free_idx(x)), $update_datas($free_idx(y)))

  clause 66 : (ARRAY.GET x _extension?{_extension <- _extension?}) = $update_types($free_idx(x))

  clause 67 : (ARRAY.SET x) = $update_types($free_idx(x))

  clause 68 : (ARRAY.LEN) = $free_empty

  clause 69 : (ARRAY.COPY x y) = $free_union($update_types($free_idx(x)), $update_types($free_idx(y)))

  clause 70 : (ARRAY.FILL x) = $update_types($free_idx(x))

  clause 71 : (ARRAY.INIT_DATA x y) = $free_union($update_types($free_idx(x)), $update_datas($free_idx(y)))

  clause 72 : (ARRAY.INIT_ELEM x y) = $free_union($update_types($free_idx(x)), $update_elems($free_idx(y)))

  clause 73 : (EXTERN.CONVERT _eop) = $free_empty

  clause 74 : (CONST _num) = $free_empty

  clause 75 : (TEST _top) = $free_empty

  clause 76 : (COMPARE _rop) = $free_empty

  clause 77 : (UNARY _uop) = $free_empty

  clause 78 : (BINOP _bop) = $free_empty

  clause 79 : (CONVERT _cop) = $free_empty

  clause -1 : (instr) = $free_empty

def $free_block(instr*) : free =

  clause 0 : (instr*{instr <- instr*}) = free'
  -- if (free = $free_list<instr>($free_instr, instr*{instr <- instr*}))
  -- if (free' = free[LABELS = $free_shift(free.LABELS)])

def $free_const(const) : free =

  clause 0 : (instr*{instr <- instr*}) = $free_block(instr*{instr <- instr*})

def $update_types(set<typeidx>) : free =

  clause 0 : (`{ x*{x <- x*} }) = $free_empty[TYPES = `{ x*{x <- x*} }]

def $update_globals(set<globalidx>) : free =

  clause 0 : (`{ x*{x <- x*} }) = $free_empty[GLOBALS = `{ x*{x <- x*} }]

def $update_tables(set<tableidx>) : free =

  clause 0 : (`{ x*{x <- x*} }) = $free_empty[TABLES = `{ x*{x <- x*} }]

def $update_mems(set<memidx>) : free =

  clause 0 : (`{ x*{x <- x*} }) = $free_empty[MEMS = `{ x*{x <- x*} }]

def $update_tags(set<tagidx>) : free =

  clause 0 : (`{ x*{x <- x*} }) = $free_empty[TAGS = `{ x*{x <- x*} }]

def $update_funcs(set<funcidx>) : free =

  clause 0 : (`{ x*{x <- x*} }) = $free_empty[FUNCS = `{ x*{x <- x*} }]

def $update_elems(set<elemidx>) : free =

  clause 0 : (`{ x*{x <- x*} }) = $free_empty[ELEMS = `{ x*{x <- x*} }]

def $update_datas(set<dataidx>) : free =

  clause 0 : (`{ x*{x <- x*} }) = $free_empty[DATAS = `{ x*{x <- x*} }]

def $update_locals(set<localidx>) : free =

  clause 0 : (`{ x*{x <- x*} }) = $free_empty[LOCALS = `{ x*{x <- x*} }]

def $update_labels(set<labelidx>) : free =

  clause 0 : (`{ x*{x <- x*} }) = $free_empty[LABELS = `{ x*{x <- x*} }]

def $update_label(idx) : free =

  clause 0 : (x) = $update_labels($free_idx(x))

def $free_tabletype(tabletype) : free =

  clause 0 : (TableT _at _lim rt) = $free_reftype(rt)

def $free_memtype(memtype) : free =

  clause 0 : (MemoryT _at _lim) = $free_empty

def $free_elemmode($f(set<tableidx>) : free, elemmode) : free =

  clause 0 : ($f, elemmode) = $free_empty
  -- if ((elemmode = Passive) \/ (elemmode = Declarative))

  clause 1 : ($f, elemmode) = $free_union(free_1, free_2)
  -- if (elemmode = Active {INDEX idx, OFFSET instr*{instr <- instr*}})
  -- if (free_1 = $f(`{ [idx] }))
  -- if (free_2 = $free_const(instr*{instr <- instr*}))

def $free_datamode($f(set<memidx>) : free, datamode) : free =

  clause 0 : ($f, datamode) = $free_empty
  -- if (datamode = Passive)

  clause 1 : ($f, datamode) = $free_union(free_1, free_2)
  -- if (datamode = Active {INDEX idx, OFFSET instr*{instr <- instr*}})
  -- if (free_1 = $f(`{ [idx] }))
  -- if (free_2 = $free_const(instr*{instr <- instr*}))

def $free_importdesc(importdesc) : free =

  clause 0 : (FuncImport x) = $update_types($free_idx(x))

  clause 1 : (TableImport tt) = $free_tabletype(tt)

  clause 2 : (MemImport mt) = $free_memtype(mt)

  clause 3 : (GlobalImport gt) = $free_globaltype(gt)

  clause 4 : (TagImport x) = $update_tags($free_idx(x))

def $free_exportdesc(exportdesc) : free =

  clause 0 : (FuncExport x) = $update_funcs($free_idx(x))

  clause 1 : (TableExport x) = $update_tables($free_idx(x))

  clause 2 : (MemExport x) = $update_mems($free_idx(x))

  clause 3 : (GlobalExport x) = $update_globals($free_idx(x))

  clause 4 : (TagExport x) = $update_tags($free_idx(x))

def $free_module(module) : free =

  clause 0 : (module) = free
  -- if (free_1 = $free_list<type>($free_type_, module.TYPES))
  -- if (free_2 = $free_list<global>($free_global, module.GLOBALS))
  -- if (free_3 = $free_list<table>($free_table, module.TABLES))
  -- if (free_4 = $free_list<memory>($free_mem, module.MEMS))
  -- if (free_5 = $free_opt<start>($free_start, module.START))
  -- if (free_6 = $free_list<elem>($free_elem, module.ELEMS))
  -- if (free_7 = $free_list<data>($free_data, module.DATAS))
  -- if (free_8 = $free_list<import>($free_import, module.IMPORTS))
  -- if (free_9 = $free_list<export>($free_export, module.EXPORTS))
  -- if (free = $free_unions([free_1, free_2, free_3, free_4, free_5, free_6, free_7, free_8, free_9]))

def $match_null(null, null) : bool =

  clause 0 : (NoNull, Null) = true

  clause 1 : (null_1, null_2) = true
  -- if (null_1 = null_2)

  clause -1 : (null_1, null_2) = false

def $subst_of(typevar, deftype*) : heaptype =

  clause 0 : (StatX x, dt*{dt <- dt*}) = DefHT dt*{dt <- dt*}[x]

  clause 1 : (RecX x, dt*{dt <- dt*}) = VarHT RecX x as heaptype

def $match_deftype(deftype*, deftype, deftype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, dt_1, dt_2) = true
  -- if (dt_1 = dt_2)

  clause 1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, dt_1, dt_2) = true
  -- if ($subst_deftype<deftype*>(dt*{dt <- dt*}, $subst_of, dt_1) = $subst_deftype<deftype*>(dt*{dt <- dt*}, $subst_of, dt_2))

  clause 2 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, dt_1, dt_2) = $match_heaptype_exists(dt*{dt <- dt*}, ht*{ht <- ht*}, dt_2)
  -- if (SubT fin ht*{ht <- ht*} strt = $unrolldt(dt_1))

def $match_heaptype(deftype*, heaptype, heaptype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, ht, AnyHT) = true
  -- if ((((ht = EqHT) \/ (ht = StructHT)) \/ (ht = ArrayHT)) \/ (ht = I31HT))

  clause 1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, ht, EqHT) = true
  -- if (((ht = I31HT) \/ (ht = StructHT)) \/ (ht = ArrayHT))

  clause 2 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, NoneHT, ht) = $match_heaptype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, ht, AnyHT)

  clause 3 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, NoFuncHT, ht) = $match_heaptype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, ht, FuncHT)

  clause 4 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, NoExnHT, ht) = $match_heaptype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, ht, ExnHT)

  clause 5 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, NoExternHT, ht) = $match_heaptype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, ht, ExternHT)

  clause 6 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, VarHT StatX x as heaptype, ht) = $match_heaptype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, DefHT dt*{dt <- dt*}[x], ht)

  clause 7 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, ht, VarHT StatX x as heaptype) = $match_heaptype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, ht, DefHT dt*{dt <- dt*}[x])

  clause 8 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, DefHT dt_1, DefHT dt_2) = $match_deftype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, dt_1, dt_2)

  clause 9 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, DefHT dt_1, ht) = true
  -- if (DefStructT _strctt = $expanddt(dt_1))
  -- if (((ht = AnyHT) \/ (ht = EqHT)) \/ (ht = StructHT))

  clause 10 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, DefHT dt_1, ht) = true
  -- if (DefArrayT _arrt = $expanddt(dt_1))
  -- if (((ht = AnyHT) \/ (ht = EqHT)) \/ (ht = ArrayHT))

  clause 11 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, DefHT dt_1, FuncHT) = true
  -- if (DefFuncT _ft = $expanddt(dt_1))

  clause 12 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, BotHT, _ht) = true

  clause 13 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, ht_1, ht_2) = true
  -- if (ht_1 = ht_2)

  clause -1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, ht_1, ht_2) = false

def $match_heaptype_exists(deftype*, heaptype*, deftype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, [], dt_1) = false

  clause 1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, ht_h :: ht_t*{ht_t <- ht_t*}, dt_1) = true
  -- if $match_heaptype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, ht_h, DefHT dt_1)

  clause 2 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, ht_h :: ht_t*{ht_t <- ht_t*}, dt_1) = $match_heaptype_exists(dt*{dt <- dt*}, ht_t*{ht_t <- ht_t*}, dt_1)
  -- if ~$match_heaptype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, ht_h, DefHT dt_1)

def $match_numtype(deftype*, numtype, numtype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, nt_1, nt_2) = true
  -- if (nt_1 = nt_2)

  clause -1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, nt_1, nt_2) = false

def $match_vectype(deftype*, vectype, vectype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, vt_1, vt_2) = (vt_1 = vt_2)

def $match_reftype(deftype*, reftype, reftype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, null_1 ht_1, null_2 ht_2) = true
  -- if ($match_null(null_1, null_2) /\ $match_heaptype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, ht_1, ht_2))

  clause -1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, _rt, _rt') = false

def $match_valtype(deftype*, valtype, valtype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, NumT nt_1, NumT nt_2) = $match_numtype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, nt_1, nt_2)

  clause 1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, VecT vt_1, VecT vt_2) = $match_vectype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, vt_1, vt_2)

  clause 2 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, RefT rt_1, RefT rt_2) = $match_reftype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, rt_1, rt_2)

  clause 3 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, BotT, _t) = true

  clause -1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, t_1, t_2) = false

def $match_packtype(deftype*, packtype, packtype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, pt_1, pt_2) = (pt_1 = pt_2)

def $match_storagetype(deftype*, storagetype, storagetype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, ValStorageT t_1, ValStorageT t_2) = $match_valtype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, t_1, t_2)

  clause 1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, PackStorageT pt_1, PackStorageT pt_2) = $match_packtype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, pt_1, pt_2)

  clause -1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, _zt, _zt') = false

def $match_arraytype(deftype*, arraytype, arraytype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, ArrayT yt_1, ArrayT yt_2) = $match_fieldtype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, yt_1, yt_2)

def $match_functype(deftype*, functype, functype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, FuncT t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}, FuncT t_1'*{t_1' <- t_1'*} t_2'*{t_2' <- t_2'*}) = ($match_resulttype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, t_1'*{t_1' <- t_1'*}, t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}) /\ $match_resulttype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}, t_2'*{t_2' <- t_2'*}))

def $match_fieldtype(deftype*, fieldtype, fieldtype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, FieldT mut_1 zt_1, FieldT mut_2 zt_2) = true
  -- if (((mut_1 = mut_2) /\ $match_storagetype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, zt_1, zt_2)) /\ (mut_1 = Cons))

  clause 1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, FieldT mut_1 zt_1, FieldT mut_2 zt_2) = true
  -- if ((((mut_1 = mut_2) /\ $match_storagetype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, zt_1, zt_2)) /\ (mut_1 = Var)) /\ $match_storagetype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, zt_2, zt_1))

  clause -1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, _yt, _yt') = false

def $match_structtype(deftype*, structtype, structtype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, StructT yt_1*{yt_1 <- yt_1*}, StructT yt_2*{yt_2 <- yt_2*}) = true
  -- if (|yt_1*{yt_1 <- yt_1*}| >= |yt_2*{yt_2 <- yt_2*}|)
  -- if (yt_1'*{yt_1' <- yt_1'*} = yt_1*{yt_1 <- yt_1*}[0 : |yt_2*{yt_2 <- yt_2*}|])
  -- (if $match_fieldtype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, yt_1', yt_2))*{yt_1' <- yt_1'*, yt_2 <- yt_2*}

  clause -1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, _strctt, _strctt') = false

def $match_strtype(deftype*, strtype, strtype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, DefStructT strctt_1, DefStructT strctt_2) = $match_structtype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, strctt_1, strctt_2)

  clause 1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, DefArrayT arrt_1, DefArrayT arrt_2) = $match_arraytype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, arrt_1, arrt_2)

  clause 2 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, DefFuncT ft_1, DefFuncT ft_2) = $match_functype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, ft_1, ft_2)

  clause -1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, strt_1, strt_2) = false

def $match_resulttype(deftype*, resulttype, resulttype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}, t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}) = true
  -- if (|t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}| = |t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}|)
  -- (if $match_valtype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, t_1, t_2))*{t_1 <- t_1*, t_2 <- t_2*}

  clause -1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, _rest, _rest') = false

def $abs_of_str_type(strtype) : heaptype =

  clause 0 : (DefStructT _strctt) = StructHT

  clause 1 : (DefArrayT _arrt) = ArrayHT

  clause 2 : (DefFuncT _ft) = FuncHT

def $top_of_str_type(deftype*, strtype) : heaptype =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, strt) = $top_of_heap_type(dt*{dt <- dt*}, $abs_of_str_type(strt))

def $top_of_heap_type(deftype*, heaptype) : heaptype =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, ht) = AnyHT
  -- if ht <- [AnyHT, NoneHT, EqHT, StructHT, ArrayHT, I31HT]

  clause 1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, ht) = FuncHT
  -- if ht <- [FuncHT, NoFuncHT]

  clause 2 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, ht) = ExnHT
  -- if ht <- [ExnHT, NoExnHT]

  clause 3 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, ht) = ExternHT
  -- if ht <- [ExternHT, NoExternHT]

  clause 4 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, DefHT dt') = $top_of_str_type(dt*{dt <- dt*}, $expanddt(dt'))

  clause 5 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, VarHT StatX x as heaptype) = $top_of_str_type(dt*{dt <- dt*}, $expanddt(dt*{dt <- dt*}[x]))

def $bot_of_str_type(strtype) : heaptype =

  clause 0 : (DefStructT _strctt) = NoneHT

  clause 1 : (DefArrayT _arrt) = NoneHT

  clause 2 : (DefFuncT _ft) = NoFuncHT

def $bot_of_heap_type(heaptype) : heaptype =

  clause 0 : (ht) = NoneHT
  -- if ht <- [AnyHT, NoneHT, EqHT, StructHT, ArrayHT, I31HT]

  clause 1 : (ht) = NoFuncHT
  -- if ht <- [FuncHT, NoFuncHT]

  clause 2 : (ht) = NoExnHT
  -- if ht <- [ExnHT, NoExnHT]

  clause 3 : (ht) = NoExternHT
  -- if ht <- [ExternHT, NoExternHT]

  clause 4 : (DefHT dt') = $bot_of_str_type($expanddt(dt'))

syntax ellipses = 
   | NoEllipses (from ellipses) 
   | Ellipses (from ellipses) 

syntax inferresulttype = (ellipses, valtype*)

syntax inferfunctype = {INS inferresulttype, OUTS inferresulttype}

syntax inferinstrtype = (inferfunctype, localidx*)

var irt : inferresulttype

var ift : inferfunctype

syntax context = {TYPES deftype*, FUNCS deftype*, TABLES tabletype*, MEMS memtype*, TAGS tagtype*, GLOBALS globaltype*, ELEMS elemtype*, DATAS datatype*, LOCALS localtype*, RESULTS valtype*, LABELS resulttype*, REFS free}

var C : context

def $empty_context : context =

  clause 0 :  = {TYPES [], FUNCS [], TABLES [], MEMS [], TAGS [], GLOBALS [], ELEMS [], DATAS [], LOCALS [], RESULTS [], LABELS [], REFS $free_empty}

relation Match_resulttype: context |- resulttype resulttype : OK

  rulegroup 

    rule : C |- t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*} : OK
    -- if (|t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}| = |t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}|)
    -- (if $match_valtype(C.TYPES, t_1, t_2))*{t_1 <- t_1*, t_2 <- t_2*}

def $stack(resulttype) : (ellipses, resulttype) =

  clause 0 : (resulttype) = (NoEllipses, resulttype)

def $pop(context, (ellipses, resulttype), (ellipses, resulttype)) : (ellipses, resulttype) =

  clause 0 : (C, (Ellipses, t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}), (Ellipses, t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*})) = (Ellipses, [])
  -- if (n_1 = |t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}|)
  -- if (n_2 = |t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}|)
  -- if (n = $min_(n_1, n_2))
  -- if (n_3 as int = (n_1 - n))
  -- if (n_4 as int = (n_2 - n))
  -- if (t_2'*{t_2' <- t_2'*} = t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}[n_4 : n])
  -- if Match_resulttype: C |- $make_<valtype>(BotT, n_3) ++ t_2'*{t_2' <- t_2'*} t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} : OK holds

  clause 1 : (C, (NoEllipses, t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}), (Ellipses, t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*})) = (Ellipses, t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}[0 : n_4])
  -- if (n_1 = |t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}|)
  -- if (n_2 = |t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}|)
  -- if (n = $min_(n_1, n_2))
  -- if (n_3 as int = (n_1 - n))
  -- if (n_4 as int = (n_2 - n))
  -- if (t_2'*{t_2' <- t_2'*} = t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}[n_4 : n])
  -- if Match_resulttype: C |- $make_<valtype>(BotT, n_3) ++ t_2'*{t_2' <- t_2'*} t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} : OK holds

  clause 2 : (C, (Ellipses, t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}), (NoEllipses, t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*})) = (NoEllipses, [])
  -- if (n_1 = |t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}|)
  -- if (n_2 = |t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}|)
  -- if (n = $min_(n_1, n_2))
  -- if (n_3 as int = (n_2 - n))
  -- if (t_2'*{t_2' <- t_2'*} = t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}[n_3 : n])
  -- if Match_resulttype: C |- t_2'*{t_2' <- t_2'*} t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} : OK holds

  clause 3 : (C, (NoEllipses, t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}), (NoEllipses, t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*})) = (NoEllipses, t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}[0 : n_3])
  -- if (n_1 = |t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}|)
  -- if (n_2 = |t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}|)
  -- if (n = $min_(n_1, n_2))
  -- if (n_3 as int = (n_2 - n))
  -- if (t_2'*{t_2' <- t_2'*} = t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}[n_3 : n])
  -- if Match_resulttype: C |- t_2'*{t_2' <- t_2'*} t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} : OK holds

def $push(context, (ellipses, resulttype), (ellipses, resulttype)) : (ellipses, resulttype) =

  clause 0 : (C, (NoEllipses, t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}), (NoEllipses, t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*})) = (NoEllipses, t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*} ++ t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*})

  clause 1 : (C, (NoEllipses, t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}), (Ellipses, t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*})) = (Ellipses, t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*} ++ t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*})

  clause 2 : (C, (Ellipses, t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}), (ellipses_2, [])) = (Ellipses, t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*})

def $peek(nat, inferresulttype) : valtype =

  clause 0 : (n, (ellipses, t*{t <- t*})) = $rev_<valtype>(t*{t <- t*})[n]
  -- if (n < |t*{t <- t*}|)

  clause -1 : (n, (ellipses, t*{t <- t*})) = BotT

def $peek_ref(nat, inferresulttype) : reftype =

  clause 0 : (n, (ellipses, t*{t <- t*})) = rt
  -- if ($peek(n, (ellipses, t*{t <- t*})) = RefT rt)

  clause 1 : (n, (ellipses, t*{t <- t*})) = NoNull BotHT
  -- if ($peek(n, (ellipses, t*{t <- t*})) = BotT)

def $local(context, typeidx) : localtype =

  clause 0 : (C, x) = C.LOCALS[x]

def $init_local(context, typeidx) : context =

  clause 0 : (C, x) = C[LOCALS[x] = LocalT Set t]
  -- if (LocalT init t = $local(C, x))

def $init_locals(context, typeidx*) : context =

  clause 0 : (C, []) = C

  clause 1 : (C, x_1 :: x*{x <- x*}) = $init_locals($init_local(C, x_1), x*{x <- x*})

def $init_valtypes(inferresulttype, nat, nat) : valtype* =

  clause 0 : (irt, i, n) = t :: $init_valtypes(irt, (i + 1), n)
  -- if (i < n)
  -- if (n' as int = (n - i))
  -- if (t = $peek(n', irt))

  clause -1 : (irt, i, n) = []

relation Numtype_ok: context |- numtype : OK

  rulegroup 

    rule : C_0 |- numtype : OK

relation Vectype_ok: context |- vectype : OK

  rulegroup 

    rule : C_0 |- vectype : OK

relation Heaptype_ok: context |- heaptype : OK

  rulegroup abs

    rule abs: C_0 |- ht : OK
    -- if ht <- [AnyHT, NoneHT, EqHT, I31HT, StructHT, ArrayHT, FuncHT, NoFuncHT, ExnHT, NoExnHT, ExternHT, NoExternHT, BotHT]

  rulegroup typeuse

    rule typeuse: C_0 |- VarHT StatX x as heaptype : OK
    -- if ((x >= 0) /\ (x < |C_0.TYPES|))

relation Reftype_ok: context |- reftype : OK

  rulegroup 

    rule : C_0 |- null ht : OK
    -- if Heaptype_ok: C_0 |- ht : OK holds

relation Valtype_ok: context |- valtype : OK

  rulegroup num

    rule num: C_0 |- NumT nt : OK
    -- if Numtype_ok: C_0 |- nt : OK holds

  rulegroup vec

    rule vec: C_0 |- VecT vt : OK
    -- if Vectype_ok: C_0 |- vt : OK holds

  rulegroup ref

    rule ref: C_0 |- RefT null ht : OK
    -- if Reftype_ok: C_0 |- null ht : OK holds

relation Resulttype_ok: context |- resulttype : OK

  rulegroup 

    rule : C_0 |- t*{t <- t*} : OK
    -- (if Valtype_ok: C_0 |- t : OK holds)*{t <- t*}

relation Functype_ok: context |- functype : OK

  rulegroup 

    rule : C_0 |- FuncT t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*} : OK
    -- if Resulttype_ok: C_0 |- t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} : OK holds
    -- if Resulttype_ok: C_0 |- t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*} : OK holds

relation Subtype_ok: context |- subtype : OK

  rulegroup 

    rule : C_0 |- subtype : OK
    -- if (SubT fin ht*{ht <- ht*} strt = subtype)
    -- (if Heaptype_ok: C_0 |- ht : OK holds)*{ht <- ht*}
    -- if Strtype_ok: C_0 |- strt : OK holds

relation Storagetype_ok: context |- storagetype : OK

  rulegroup val

    rule val: C_0 |- ValStorageT t : OK
    -- if Valtype_ok: C_0 |- t : OK holds

  rulegroup pack

    rule pack: C_0 |- PackStorageT pt : OK
    -- if pt <- [I8, I16]

relation Fieldtype_ok: context |- fieldtype : OK

  rulegroup 

    rule : C_0 |- FieldT mut zt : OK
    -- if Storagetype_ok: C_0 |- zt : OK holds

relation Structtype_ok: context |- structtype : OK

  rulegroup 

    rule : C_0 |- StructT yt*{yt <- yt*} : OK
    -- (if Fieldtype_ok: C_0 |- yt : OK holds)*{yt <- yt*}

relation Arraytype_ok: context |- arraytype : OK

  rulegroup 

    rule : C_0 |- ArrayT yt : OK
    -- if Fieldtype_ok: C_0 |- yt : OK holds

relation Strtype_ok: context |- strtype : OK

  rulegroup struct

    rule struct: C_0 |- DefStructT strctt : OK
    -- if Structtype_ok: C_0 |- strctt : OK holds

  rulegroup array

    rule array: C_0 |- DefArrayT arrt : OK
    -- if Arraytype_ok: C_0 |- arrt : OK holds

  rulegroup func

    rule func: C_0 |- DefFuncT ft : OK
    -- if Functype_ok: C_0 |- ft : OK holds

relation Subtype_sub_ok: context typeidx |- subtype : OK

  rulegroup 

    rule : C_0 x |- subtype : OK
    -- if (SubT fin ht*{ht <- ht*} strt = subtype)
    -- (if Subtype_sub_ok': C_0 x strt |- ht : OK holds)*{ht <- ht*}

relation Subtype_sub_ok': context typeidx strtype |- heaptype : OK

  rulegroup 

    rule : C_0 x strt |- VarHT StatX x' as heaptype : OK
    -- if (SubT fin _ht*{_ht <- _ht*} strt' = $unrolldt(C_0.TYPES[x']))
    -- if (((x' < x) /\ (fin = NoFinal)) /\ $match_strtype(C_0.TYPES, strt, strt'))

relation Rectype_ok: context |- rectype : context

  rulegroup 

    rule : C_0 |- rectype : C_1
    -- if (RecT st*{st <- st*} = rectype)
    -- if (x = |C_0.TYPES|)
    -- if (n*{n <- n*} = $range(|st*{st <- st*}|))
    -- if (dt*{dt <- dt*} = $rolldt(x, rectype))
    -- if (C_1 = C_0[TYPES = C_0.TYPES ++ dt*{dt <- dt*}])
    -- (if Subtype_ok: C_1 |- st : OK holds)*{st <- st*}
    -- (if Subtype_sub_ok: C_1 (x + n) |- st : OK holds)*{n <- n*, st <- st*}

relation Memtype_ok: context |- memtype : OK

  rulegroup i32

    rule i32: C_0 |- MemoryT I32AT lim : OK
    -- if Limits_ok: C_0 65536 as range |- lim : OK holds

  rulegroup i64

    rule i64: C_0 |- MemoryT I64AT lim : OK
    -- if Limits_ok: C_0 281474976710656 as range |- lim : OK holds

relation Globaltype_ok: context |- globaltype : OK

  rulegroup 

    rule : C_0 |- GlobalT mut t : OK
    -- if Valtype_ok: C_0 |- t : OK holds

relation Limits_ok: context range |- limits : OK

  rulegroup without-max

    rule without-max: C_0 range |- {MIN i64, MAX ?()} : OK
    -- if $le_u(i64, range)

  rulegroup with-max

    rule with-max: C_0 range |- {MIN i64, MAX ?(i64')} : OK
    -- if (($le_u(i64, range) /\ $le_u(i64', range)) /\ $le_u(i64, i64'))

relation Tabletype_ok: context |- tabletype : OK

  rulegroup i32

    rule i32: C_0 |- TableT I32AT lim rt : OK
    -- if Reftype_ok: C_0 |- rt : OK holds
    -- if Limits_ok: C_0 4294967295 as range |- lim : OK holds

  rulegroup i64

    rule i64: C_0 |- TableT I64AT lim rt : OK
    -- if Reftype_ok: C_0 |- rt : OK holds
    -- if Limits_ok: C_0 18446744073709551615 as range |- lim : OK holds

relation Blocktype_ok: context |- blocktype : instrtype

  rulegroup valblocktype.none

    rule valblocktype.none: C_0 |- ValBlockType ?() : InstrT [] [] []

  rulegroup valblocktype.some

    rule valblocktype.some: C_0 |- ValBlockType ?(t) : InstrT [] [t] []
    -- if Valtype_ok: C_0 |- t : OK holds

  rulegroup varblocktype

    rule varblocktype: C_0 |- VarBlockType x : InstrT t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*} []
    -- if (FuncT t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*} = $func_type(C_0, x))

def $array_type(context, typeidx) : arraytype =

  clause 0 : (C, x) = arrt
  -- if (DefArrayT arrt = $expanddt(C.TYPES[x]))

def $struct_type(context, typeidx) : structtype =

  clause 0 : (C, x) = strctt
  -- if (DefStructT strctt = $expanddt(C.TYPES[x]))

def $func_type(context, typeidx) : functype =

  clause 0 : (C, x) = ft
  -- if (DefFuncT ft = $expanddt(C.TYPES[x]))

relation Catch_ok: context resulttype |- catch : OK

  rulegroup catch

    rule catch: C_0 t*{t <- t*} |- Catch x y : OK
    -- if (TagT dt = C_0.TAGS[x])
    -- if (FuncT t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*} = $functype_of_strtype($expanddt(dt)))
    -- if $match_resulttype(C_0.TYPES, t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}, C_0.LABELS[y])

  rulegroup catch_ref

    rule catch_ref: C_0 t*{t <- t*} |- CatchRef x y : OK
    -- if (TagT dt = C_0.TAGS[x])
    -- if (FuncT t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*} = $functype_of_strtype($expanddt(dt)))
    -- if $match_resulttype(C_0.TYPES, t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} ++ [RefT NoNull ExnHT], C_0.LABELS[y])

  rulegroup catch_all

    rule catch_all: C_0 t*{t <- t*} |- CatchAll x : OK
    -- if $match_resulttype(C_0.TYPES, [], C_0.LABELS[x])

  rulegroup catch_all_ref

    rule catch_all_ref: C_0 t*{t <- t*} |- CatchAllRef x : OK
    -- if $match_resulttype(C_0.TYPES, [RefT NoNull ExnHT], C_0.LABELS[x])

relation Instrs_ok: context inferresulttype |- instr* : (inferresulttype, idx*)

  rulegroup empty

    rule empty: C_0 irt |- [] : (irt, [])

  rulegroup cons

    rule cons: C_0 irt |- instr_1 :: instr*{instr <- instr*} : (irt', idx'*{idx' <- idx'*})
    -- Instr_ok: C_0 irt |- instr_1 : ({INS irt_1, OUTS irt_2}, idx*{idx <- idx*})
    -- if (C_1 = $init_locals(C_0, idx*{idx <- idx*}))
    -- if ((ellipses, valtype*{valtype <- valtype*}) = $push(C_0, irt_2, $pop(C_0, irt_1, irt)))
    -- Instrs_ok: C_1 (ellipses, valtype*{valtype <- valtype*}) |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : (irt', idx'*{idx' <- idx'*})

relation Instr_ok: context inferresulttype |- instr : inferinstrtype

  rulegroup unreachable

    rule unreachable: C_0 irt |- UNREACHABLE : ({INS (Ellipses, []), OUTS (Ellipses, [])}, [])

  rulegroup nop

    rule nop: C_0 irt |- NOP : ({INS (NoEllipses, []), OUTS (NoEllipses, [])}, [])

  rulegroup drop

    rule drop: C_0 irt |- DROP : ({INS (NoEllipses, [$peek(0, irt)]), OUTS (NoEllipses, [])}, [])

  rulegroup select-none

    rule select-none: C_0 irt |- SELECT ?() : ({INS (NoEllipses, [t, t, NumT I32T]), OUTS (NoEllipses, [t])}, [])
    -- if (t = $peek(1, irt))
    -- if ($is_num_type(t) \/ $is_vec_type(t))

  rulegroup select-some

    rule select-some: C_0 irt |- SELECT ?(t*{t <- t*}) : ({INS (NoEllipses, t*{t <- t*} ++ t*{t <- t*} ++ [NumT I32T]), OUTS (NoEllipses, t*{t <- t*})}, [])
    -- if (|t*{t <- t*}| = 1)
    -- if Resulttype_ok: C_0 |- t*{t <- t*} : OK holds

  rulegroup block

    rule block: C_0 irt |- BLOCK blocktype instr*{instr <- instr*} : ({INS (NoEllipses, t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}), OUTS (NoEllipses, t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*})}, x*{x <- x*})
    -- Blocktype_ok: C_0 |- blocktype : InstrT t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*} x*{x <- x*}
    -- if Block_ok: C_0[LABELS = [t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}] ++ C_0.LABELS] InstrT t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*} x*{x <- x*} |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : OK holds

  rulegroup loop

    rule loop: C_0 irt |- LOOP blocktype instr*{instr <- instr*} : ({INS (NoEllipses, t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}), OUTS (NoEllipses, t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*})}, x*{x <- x*})
    -- Blocktype_ok: C_0 |- blocktype : InstrT t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*} x*{x <- x*}
    -- if Block_ok: C_0[LABELS = [t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}] ++ C_0.LABELS] InstrT t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*} x*{x <- x*} |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : OK holds

  rulegroup if'

    rule if': C_0 irt |- IF blocktype instr_t*{instr_t <- instr_t*} ELSE instr_f*{instr_f <- instr_f*} : ({INS (NoEllipses, t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} ++ [NumT I32T]), OUTS (NoEllipses, t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*})}, x*{x <- x*})
    -- Blocktype_ok: C_0 |- blocktype : InstrT t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*} x*{x <- x*}
    -- if Block_ok: C_0[LABELS = [t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}] ++ C_0.LABELS] InstrT t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*} x*{x <- x*} |- instr_t*{instr_t <- instr_t*} : OK holds
    -- if Block_ok: C_0[LABELS = [t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}] ++ C_0.LABELS] InstrT t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*} x*{x <- x*} |- instr_f*{instr_f <- instr_f*} : OK holds

  rulegroup br

    rule br: C_0 irt |- BR x : ({INS (Ellipses, C_0.LABELS[x]), OUTS (Ellipses, [])}, [])

  rulegroup br_if

    rule br_if: C_0 irt |- BR_IF x : ({INS (NoEllipses, t*{t <- t*} ++ [NumT I32T]), OUTS (NoEllipses, t*{t <- t*})}, [])
    -- if (t*{t <- t*} = C_0.LABELS[x])

  rulegroup br_table

    -- if (t = $unpacked_storage_type(zt))

  rulegroup array.len

    rule array.len: C_0 irt |- ARRAY.LEN : ({INS (NoEllipses, [RefT Null ArrayHT]), OUTS (NoEllipses, [NumT I32T])}, [])

  rulegroup array.copy

    rule array.copy: C_0 irt |- ARRAY.COPY x y : ({INS (NoEllipses, [RefT Null DefHT C_0.TYPES[x], NumT I32T, RefT Null DefHT C_0.TYPES[y], NumT I32T, NumT I32T]), OUTS (NoEllipses, [])}, [])
    -- if (ArrayT FieldT mut_1 zt_1 = $array_type(C_0, x))
    -- if (ArrayT FieldT mut_2 zt_2 = $array_type(C_0, y))
    -- if (mut_1 = Var)
    -- if $match_storagetype(C_0.TYPES, zt_2, zt_1)

  rulegroup array.fill

    rule array.fill: C_0 irt |- ARRAY.FILL x : ({INS (NoEllipses, [RefT Null DefHT C_0.TYPES[x], NumT I32T, t, NumT I32T]), OUTS (NoEllipses, [])}, [])
    -- if (ArrayT FieldT mut zt = $array_type(C_0, x))
    -- if (mut = Var)
    -- if (t = $unpacked_storage_type(zt))

  rulegroup array.init.data

    rule array.init.data: C_0 irt |- ARRAY.INIT_DATA x y : ({INS (NoEllipses, [RefT Null DefHT C_0.TYPES[x], NumT I32T, NumT I32T, NumT I32T]), OUTS (NoEllipses, [])}, [])
    -- if (ArrayT FieldT mut zt = $array_type(C_0, x))
    -- if (mut = Var)
    -- if (C_0.DATAS[y] = OK)
    -- if (t = $unpacked_storage_type(zt))
    -- if ($is_num_type(t) \/ $is_vec_type(t))

  rulegroup array.init.elem

    rule array.init.elem: C_0 irt |- ARRAY.INIT_ELEM x y : ({INS (NoEllipses, [RefT Null DefHT C_0.TYPES[x], NumT I32T, NumT I32T, NumT I32T]), OUTS (NoEllipses, [])}, [])
    -- if (ArrayT FieldT mut zt = $array_type(C_0, x))
    -- if (mut = Var)
    -- if (rt = C_0.ELEMS[y])
    -- if $match_valtype(C_0.TYPES, RefT rt, $unpacked_storage_type(zt))

  rulegroup extern.convert

    rule extern.convert: C_0 irt |- EXTERN.CONVERT eop : ({INS (NoEllipses, [RefT null ht_1]), OUTS (NoEllipses, [RefT null ht_2])}, [])
    -- if ((ht_1, ht_2) = $type_externop(eop))
    -- if (null ht = $peek_ref(0, irt))

  rulegroup const

    rule const: C_0 irt |- CONST num : ({INS (NoEllipses, []), OUTS (NoEllipses, [t])}, [])
    -- if (t = NumT $type_num<i32, i64, f32, f64>(num))

  rulegroup testop

    rule testop: C_0 irt |- TEST top : ({INS (NoEllipses, [t]), OUTS (NoEllipses, [NumT I32T])}, [])
    -- if (t = NumT $type_num<itestop, itestop, ftestop, ftestop>(top))

  rulegroup relop

    rule relop: C_0 irt |- COMPARE rop : ({INS (NoEllipses, [t, t]), OUTS (NoEllipses, [NumT I32T])}, [])
    -- if (t = NumT $type_num<irelop, irelop, frelop, frelop>(rop))

  rulegroup unop

    rule unop: C_0 irt |- UNARY uop : ({INS (NoEllipses, [t]), OUTS (NoEllipses, [t])}, [])
    -- if Unop_ok: |- uop : OK holds
    -- if (t = NumT $type_num<iunop, iunop, funop, funop>(uop))

  rulegroup binop

    rule binop: C_0 irt |- BINOP bop : ({INS (NoEllipses, [t, t]), OUTS (NoEllipses, [t])}, [])
    -- if (t = NumT $type_num<ibinop, ibinop, fbinop, fbinop>(bop))

  rulegroup cvtop

    rule cvtop: C_0 irt |- CONVERT cop : ({INS (NoEllipses, [NumT nt_1]), OUTS (NoEllipses, [NumT nt_2])}, [])
    -- if ((nt_1, nt_2) = $type_cvtop(cop))

  rulegroup vec.const

    rule vec.const: C_0 irt |- VEC.CONST vec : ({INS (NoEllipses, []), OUTS (NoEllipses, [t])}, [])
    -- if (t = VecT $type_vecop<v128>(vec))

  rulegroup vec.test

    -- if (t = VecT $type_vecop<vtestop>(vtop))

  rulegroup vec.unary

    -- if (t = VecT $type_vecop<vunop>(vuop))

  rulegroup vec.binary

    -- if Vecbinop_ok: |- vbop : OK holds
    -- if (t = VecT $type_vecop<vbinop>(vbop))

  rulegroup vec.compare

    -- if (t = VecT $type_vecop<vrelop>(vrop))

  rulegroup vec.convert

    -- if (t = VecT $type_vecop<vcvtop>(vcvop))

    -- if (t = VecT $type_vecop<vshiftop>(vshop))

    -- if (t_1 = NumT $type_vec_lane<vnsplatop, vnsplatop, vnsplatop, vnsplatop, vnsplatop, vnsplatop>(vspop))
    -- if (t_2 = VecT $type_vecop<vsplatop>(vspop))

  rulegroup vec.extract

    rule vec.extract: C_0 irt |- VEC.EXTRACT vexop : ({INS (NoEllipses, [t_1]), OUTS (NoEllipses, [t_2])}, [])
    -- if (t_1 = VecT $type_vecop<vextractop>(vexop))
    -- if (t_2 = NumT $type_vec_lane<vnextractop<extension>, vnextractop<extension>, vnextractop<void>, vnextractop<void>, vnextractop<void>, vnextractop<void>>(vexop))
    -- if ($lane_extractop(vexop) < $num_lanes<vnextractop<extension>, vnextractop<extension>, vnextractop<void>, vnextractop<void>, vnextractop<void>, vnextractop<void>>(vexop))

  rulegroup vec.replace

    rule vec.replace: C_0 irt |- VEC.REPLACE vrepop : ({INS (NoEllipses, [t_1, t_2]), OUTS (NoEllipses, [t_1])}, [])
    -- if (t_1 = VecT $type_vecop<vreplaceop>(vrepop))
    -- if (t_2 = NumT $type_vec_lane<vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop>(vrepop))

relation Block_ok: context instrtype |- const : OK

  rulegroup 

    rule : C_0 InstrT t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*} x*{x <- x*} |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : OK
    -- Instrs_ok: C_0 $stack(t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}) |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : (irt, x'*{x' <- x'*})
    -- if ((ellipses, t'*{t' <- t'*}) = $pop(C_0, $stack(t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}), irt))
    -- if (t'*{t' <- t'*} = [])

relation Pack_ok: int |- packtype : OK

  rulegroup 

    rule : i as int |- packtype : OK
    -- if ($pack_size(packtype) < i as int)

relation Unop_ok: |- unop_ : OK

  rulegroup i32

    rule i32: |- uop : OK
    -- if (uop = I32 ExtendS packtype)
    -- if Pack_ok: $num_size($type_num<iunop, iunop, funop, funop>(uop)) |- packtype : OK holds

  rulegroup i64

    rule i64: |- uop : OK
    -- if (uop = I64 ExtendS packtype)
    -- if Pack_ok: $num_size($type_num<iunop, iunop, funop, funop>(uop)) |- packtype : OK holds

  rulegroup else

    rule else: |- uop : OK

def $check_lane_index(nat*) : bool =

  clause 0 : ([]) = true

  clause 1 : (i_1 :: i*{i <- i*}) = $check_lane_index(i*{i <- i*})
  -- if (i_1 < 32)

  clause -1 : (_nat*{_nat <- _nat*}) = false

def $check_vbop_shuffle(vbinop_) : bool =

  clause 0 : (V128 I8x16 Shuffle _nat*{_nat <- _nat*}) = true

  clause -1 : (vbop) = false

relation Vecbinop_ok: |- vbinop_ : OK

  rulegroup i816

    rule i816: |- vbop : OK
    -- if (vbop = V128 I8x16 Shuffle i*{i <- i*})
    -- if $check_lane_index(i*{i <- i*})

  rulegroup else

    rule else: |- vbop : OK
    -- if ~$check_vbop_shuffle(vbop)

    rule : C_0 |- {GTYPE gt, GINIT const} : C_1
    -- if (GlobalT mut t = gt)
    -- if Globaltype_ok: C_0 |- gt : OK holds
    -- if Const_ok: C_0 t |- const : OK holds
    -- if (C_1 = C_0[GLOBALS = C_0.GLOBALS ++ [gt]])

relation Globals_ok: context |- global* : context

  rulegroup cons

    rule cons: C_0 |- global_1 :: global*{global <- global*} : C_2
    -- Global_ok: C_0 |- global_1 : C_1
    -- Globals_ok: C_1 |- global*{global <- global*} : C_2

  rulegroup nil

    rule nil: C_0 |- [] : C_0

relation Tag_ok: context |- tag : context

  rulegroup 

    rule : C_0 |- {TGTYPE x} : C_1
    -- if (FuncT _rest [] = $func_type(C_0, x))
    -- if (C_1 = C_0[TAGS = C_0.TAGS ++ [TagT C_0.TYPES[x]]])

relation Tags_ok: context |- tag* : context

  rulegroup cons

    rule cons: C_0 |- tag_1 :: tag*{tag <- tag*} : C_2
    -- Tag_ok: C_0 |- tag_1 : C_1
    -- Tags_ok: C_1 |- tag*{tag <- tag*} : C_2

  rulegroup nil

    rule nil: C_0 |- [] : C_0

relation Elem_ok: context |- elem : context

  rulegroup 

    rule : C_0 |- {ETYPE rt, EINIT const*{const <- const*}, EMODE elemmode} : C_1
    -- if Reftype_ok: C_0 |- rt : OK holds
    -- (if Const_ok: C_0 RefT rt |- const : OK holds)*{const <- const*}
    -- if Elemmode_ok: C_0 rt |- elemmode : OK holds
    -- if (C_1 = C_0[ELEMS = C_0.ELEMS ++ [rt]])

relation Elems_ok: context |- elem* : context

  rulegroup cons

    rule cons: C_0 |- elem_1 :: elem*{elem <- elem*} : C_2
    -- Elem_ok: C_0 |- elem_1 : C_1
    -- Elems_ok: C_1 |- elem*{elem <- elem*} : C_2

  rulegroup nil

    rule nil: C_0 |- [] : C_0

relation Elemmode_ok: context reftype |- elemmode : OK

  rulegroup passive

    rule passive: C_0 rt |- Passive : OK

  rulegroup active

    rule active: C_0 rt |- Active {INDEX x, OFFSET const} : OK
    -- if (TableT at lim rt' = C_0.TABLES[x])
    -- if $match_reftype(C_0.TYPES, rt, rt')
    -- if Const_ok: C_0 NumT $numtype_of_addrtype(at) |- const : OK holds

  rulegroup declarative

    rule declarative: C_0 rt |- Declarative : OK

relation Data_ok: context |- data : context

  rulegroup 

    rule : C_0 |- {DINIT _text, DMODE datamode} : C_1
    -- if Datamode_ok: C_0 |- datamode : OK holds
    -- if (C_1 = C_0[DATAS = C_0.DATAS ++ [OK]])

relation Datas_ok: context |- data* : context

  rulegroup cons

    rule cons: C_0 |- data_1 :: data*{data <- data*} : C_2
    -- Data_ok: C_0 |- data_1 : C_1
    -- Datas_ok: C_1 |- data*{data <- data*} : C_2

  rulegroup nil

    rule nil: C_0 |- [] : C_0

relation Datamode_ok: context |- datamode : OK

  rulegroup passive

    rule passive: C_0 |- Passive : OK

  rulegroup active

    rule active: C_0 |- Active {INDEX x, OFFSET const} : OK
    -- if (MemoryT at _lim = C_0.MEMS[x])
    -- if Const_ok: C_0 NumT $numtype_of_addrtype(at) |- const : OK holds

relation Start_ok: context |- start? : OK

  rulegroup some

    rule some: C_0 |- ?({SFUNC x}) : OK
    -- if (FuncT [] [] = $functype_of_strtype($expanddt(C_0.FUNCS[x])))

  rulegroup none

    rule none: C_0 |- ?() : OK

relation Exports_ok: context nameset |- export* : nameset

  rulegroup cons

    rule cons: C_0 nameset_0 |- export_1 :: export*{export <- export*} : nameset_2
    -- Export_ok: C_0 nameset_0 |- export_1 : nameset_1
    -- Exports_ok: C_0 nameset_1 |- export*{export <- export*} : nameset_2

  rulegroup nil

    rule nil: C_0 nameset_0 |- [] : nameset_0

relation Export_ok: context nameset |- export : nameset

  rulegroup func

    rule func: C_0 nameset |- {NAME name, EDESC FuncExport x} : $union_set<name>(nameset, `{ [name] })
    -- if ((x >= 0) /\ (x < |C_0.FUNCS|))
    -- if ~$in_set<name>(name, nameset)

  rulegroup global

    rule global: C_0 nameset |- {NAME name, EDESC GlobalExport x} : $union_set<name>(nameset, `{ [name] })
    -- if ((x >= 0) /\ (x < |C_0.GLOBALS|))
    -- if ~$in_set<name>(name, nameset)

  rulegroup table

    rule table: C_0 nameset |- {NAME name, EDESC TableExport x} : $union_set<name>(nameset, `{ [name] })
    -- if ((x >= 0) /\ (x < |C_0.TABLES|))
    -- if ~$in_set<name>(name, nameset)

  rulegroup mem

    rule mem: C_0 nameset |- {NAME name, EDESC MemExport x} : $union_set<name>(nameset, `{ [name] })
    -- if ((x >= 0) /\ (x < |C_0.MEMS|))
    -- if ~$in_set<name>(name, nameset)

  rulegroup tag

    rule tag: C_0 nameset |- {NAME name, EDESC TagExport x} : $union_set<name>(nameset, `{ [name] })
    -- if ((x >= 0) /\ (x < |C_0.TAGS|))
    -- if ~$in_set<name>(name, nameset)

relation Const_ok: context valtype |- const : OK

  rulegroup 

    rule : C_0 t |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : OK
    -- (if $is_const(C_0, instr))*{instr <- instr*}
    -- if Block_ok: C_0 InstrT [] [t] [] |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : OK holds

relation Module_ok: |- module : OK

  rulegroup 

    rule : |- module : OK
    -- if (module' = module[FUNCS = []][START = ?()])
    -- if (free = $free_module(module'))
    -- if (C = $empty_context[REFS = free])
    -- Types_ok: C |- module.TYPES : C_0
    -- Imports_ok: C_0 |- module.IMPORTS : C_1
    -- Funcs_ok: C_1 |- module.FUNCS : C_2
    -- Tables_ok: C_2 |- module.TABLES : C_3
    -- Mems_ok: C_3 |- module.MEMS : C_4
    -- Globals_ok: C_4 |- module.GLOBALS : C_5
    -- Tags_ok: C_5 |- module.TAGS : C_6
    -- Elems_ok: C_6 |- module.ELEMS : C_7
    -- Datas_ok: C_7 |- module.DATAS : C_8
    -- if (func*{func <- func*} = module.FUNCS)
    -- (if Funcbody_ok: C_8 |- func : OK holds)*{func <- func*}
    -- if Start_ok: C_8 |- module.START : OK holds
    -- Exports_ok: C_8 `{ [] } |- module.EXPORTS : nameset

relation Modules_ok: |- module* : OK

  rulegroup cons

    rule cons: |- module*{module <- module*} : OK
    -- (if Module_ok: |- module : OK holds)*{module <- module*}

  rulegroup nil

    rule nil: |- [] : OK

relation Local_ok: context |- local : localtype

  rulegroup set

    rule set: C |- {LTYPE t} : LocalT Set t
    -- if Valtype_ok: C |- t : OK holds
    -- if ($defaultable(t) = true)

  rulegroup unset

    rule unset: C |- {LTYPE t} : LocalT Unset t
    -- if Valtype_ok: C |- t : OK holds
    -- if ($defaultable(t) = false)

def $is_const(context, instr) : bool =

  clause 0 : (C, instr) = true
  -- if instr <- [BINOP I32 IAdd, BINOP I32 ISub, BINOP I32 IMul, BINOP I64 IAdd, BINOP I64 ISub, BINOP I64 IMul]

  clause 1 : (C, CONST _num) = true

  clause 2 : (C, VEC.CONST _vec) = true

  clause 3 : (C, REF.NULL _ht) = true

  clause 4 : (C, REF.FUNC _funcidx) = true

  clause 5 : (C, REF.I31) = true

  clause 6 : (C, STRUCT.NEW _ti _iop) = true

  clause 7 : (C, ARRAY.NEW _ti _iop) = true

  clause 8 : (C, ARRAY.NEW_FIXED _ti _u32) = true

  clause 9 : (C, GLOBAL.GET x) = true
  -- if (GlobalT mut t = C.GLOBALS[x])
  -- if (mut = Cons)

  clause -1 : (C, instr) = false

syntax byte = nat

syntax addr = nat

syntax funcaddr = addr

syntax globaladdr = addr

syntax tableaddr = addr

syntax memaddr = addr

syntax tagaddr = addr

syntax elemaddr = addr

syntax dataaddr = addr

syntax hostaddr = addr

syntax structaddr = addr

syntax arrayaddr = addr

syntax exnaddr = addr

syntax externaddr = 
   | FuncAddr funcaddr (from externaddr) 
   | GlobalAddr globaladdr (from externaddr) 
   | TableAddr tableaddr (from externaddr) 
   | MemAddr memaddr (from externaddr) 
   | TagAddr tagaddr (from externaddr) 

syntax refval = 
   | NullRef heaptype (from refval) 
   | FuncRef funcaddr (from refval) 
   | ExternRef refval (from refval) 
   | I31Ref nat (from refval) 
   | StructRef structaddr (from refval) 
   | ArrayRef arrayaddr (from refval) 
   | ExnRef exnaddr (from refval) 
   | HostRef hostaddr (from refval) 

syntax value = 
   | NumV num_ (from value) 
   | VecV vec_ (from value) 
   | RefV refval (from value) 

syntax result = 
   | Values value* (from result) 
   | Trap (from result) 

syntax hostfunc = 
   | HostFuncId hostaddr (from hostfunc) 

syntax funccode = 
   | AstFunc func (from funccode) 
   | HostFunc hostfunc (from funccode) 

syntax exportinst = {NAME name, ADDR externaddr}

syntax moduleinst = {TYPES deftype*, FUNCS funcaddr*, GLOBALS globaladdr*, TABLES tableaddr*, MEMS memaddr*, TAGS tagaddr*, ELEMS elemaddr*, DATAS dataaddr*, EXPORTS exportinst*}

syntax funcinst = {TYPE deftype, MODULE moduleinst, CODE funccode}

syntax globalinst = {TYPE globaltype, VALUE value}

syntax tableinst = {TYPE tabletype, REFS refval*}

syntax meminst = {TYPE memtype, BYTES byte*}

syntax taginst = {TYPE tagtype}

syntax eleminst = {TYPE elemtype, REFS refval*}

syntax datainst = {BYTES byte*}

syntax fieldval = 
   | FieldValue value (from fieldval) 
   | PackedField packtype nat (from fieldval) 

syntax structinst = {TYPE deftype, FIELDS fieldval*}

syntax arrayinst = {TYPE deftype, FIELDS fieldval*}

syntax exninst = {TAG tagaddr, FIELDS value*}

syntax store = {FUNCS funcinst*, GLOBALS globalinst*, TABLES tableinst*, MEMS meminst*, TAGS taginst*, ELEMS eleminst*, DATAS datainst*, STRUCTS structinst*, ARRAYS arrayinst*, EXNS exninst*}

syntax frame = {LOCALS value?*, MODULE moduleinst}

syntax state = (store, frame)

syntax config = (state, instr*)

syntax execresult = 
   | NormalR store frame value* (from execresult) 
   | BreakR store frame nat value* (from execresult) 
   | ReturnR store frame value* (from execresult) 
   | ThrowR store frame tagaddr value* (from execresult)

def $page_size : nat =

  clause 0 :  = 65536

def $empty_store : store =

  clause 0 :  = {FUNCS [], GLOBALS [], TABLES [], MEMS [], TAGS [], ELEMS [], DATAS [], STRUCTS [], ARRAYS [], EXNS []}

def $empty_moduleinst : moduleinst =

  clause 0 :  = {TYPES [], FUNCS [], GLOBALS [], TABLES [], MEMS [], TAGS [], ELEMS [], DATAS [], EXPORTS []}

def $range_from(nat, nat) : nat* =

  clause 0 : (_nat, n) = []
  -- if (n = 0)

  clause 1 : (base, n) = base :: $range_from(base', n')
  -- if (n > 0)
  -- if (base' = (base + 1))
  -- if (n' as int = (n - 1))

def $limits_min_nat(limits) : nat =

  clause 0 : ({MIN i64, MAX _fail64?{_fail64 <- _fail64?}}) = n
  -- if (i64 >= 0 as int)
  -- if (n as i64 = i64)

def $nat_of_num(num_) : nat =

  clause 0 : (I32 n) = n

  clause 1 : (I64 i as i64) = n
  -- if (n = i)

def $addr_plus_offset(num_, int) : nat =

  clause 0 : (num, int_1) = addrval
  -- if (base = $nat_of_num(num))
  -- if (int_1 >= 0 as int)
  -- if (offset as int = int_1)
  -- if (addrval = (base + offset))
