def $externaddr_funcaddr(externaddr*) : funcaddr* =

  clause 0 : ([]) = []

  clause 1 : (FuncAddr a :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = a :: $externaddr_funcaddr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

  clause 2 : (GlobalAddr _ga :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = $externaddr_funcaddr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

  clause 3 : (TableAddr _ta :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = $externaddr_funcaddr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

  clause 4 : (MemAddr _ma :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = $externaddr_funcaddr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

  clause 5 : (TagAddr _ja :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = $externaddr_funcaddr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

def $externaddr_globaladdr(externaddr*) : globaladdr* =

  clause 0 : ([]) = []

  clause 1 : (GlobalAddr a :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = a :: $externaddr_globaladdr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

  clause 2 : (FuncAddr _fa :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = $externaddr_globaladdr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

  clause 3 : (TableAddr _ta :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = $externaddr_globaladdr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

  clause 4 : (MemAddr _ma :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = $externaddr_globaladdr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

  clause 5 : (TagAddr _ja :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = $externaddr_globaladdr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

def $externaddr_tableaddr(externaddr*) : tableaddr* =

  clause 0 : ([]) = []

  clause 1 : (TableAddr a :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = a :: $externaddr_tableaddr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

  clause 2 : (FuncAddr _fa :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = $externaddr_tableaddr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

  clause 3 : (GlobalAddr _ga :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = $externaddr_tableaddr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

  clause 4 : (MemAddr _ma :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = $externaddr_tableaddr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

  clause 5 : (TagAddr _ja :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = $externaddr_tableaddr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

def $externaddr_memaddr(externaddr*) : memaddr* =

  clause 0 : ([]) = []

  clause 1 : (MemAddr a :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = a :: $externaddr_memaddr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

  clause 2 : (FuncAddr _fa :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = $externaddr_memaddr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

  clause 3 : (GlobalAddr _ga :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = $externaddr_memaddr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

  clause 4 : (TableAddr _ta :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = $externaddr_memaddr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

  clause 5 : (TagAddr _ja :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = $externaddr_memaddr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

def $externaddr_tagaddr(externaddr*) : tagaddr* =

  clause 0 : ([]) = []

  clause 1 : (TagAddr a :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = a :: $externaddr_tagaddr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

  clause 2 : (FuncAddr _fa :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = $externaddr_tagaddr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

  clause 3 : (GlobalAddr _ga :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = $externaddr_tagaddr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

  clause 4 : (TableAddr _ta :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = $externaddr_tagaddr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

  clause 5 : (MemAddr _ma :: xa*{xa <- xa*}) = $externaddr_tagaddr(xa*{xa <- xa*})

def $default_value(valtype) : value? =

  clause 0 : (NumT I32T) = ?(NumV I32 0)

  clause 1 : (NumT I64T) = ?(NumV I64 0 as i64)

  clause 2 : (NumT F32T) = ?(NumV F32 POS SUBNORM 0)

  clause 3 : (NumT F64T) = ?(NumV F64 POS SUBNORM 0)

  clause 4 : (VecT V128T) = ?(VecV V128 0)

  clause 5 : (RefT Null ht) = ?(RefV NullRef ht)

  clause 6 : (RefT NoNull _ht) = ?()

  clause 7 : (BotT) = ?()

relation Num_ok: store |- num_ : numtype

  rulegroup i32

    rule i32: s |- I32 _i32 : I32T

  rulegroup i64

    rule i64: s |- I64 _fail64 : I64T

  rulegroup f32

    rule f32: s |- F32 _f32 : F32T

  rulegroup f64

    rule f64: s |- F64 _f64 : F64T

relation Vec_ok: store |- vec_ : vectype

  rulegroup v128

    rule v128: s |- V128 _v128 : V128T

relation Ref_ok: store |- refval : reftype

  rulegroup null

    rule null: s |- NullRef ht : Null $bot_of_heap_type(ht)

  rulegroup func

    rule func: s |- FuncRef a : NoNull DefHT s.FUNCS[a].TYPE

  rulegroup i31

    rule i31: s |- I31Ref _nat : NoNull I31HT

  rulegroup struct

    rule struct: s |- StructRef a : NoNull DefHT s.STRUCTS[a].TYPE

  rulegroup array

    rule array: s |- ArrayRef a : NoNull DefHT s.ARRAYS[a].TYPE

  rulegroup exn

    rule exn: s |- ExnRef _exnaddr : NoNull ExnHT

  rulegroup host

    rule host: s |- HostRef _hostaddr : NoNull AnyHT

  rulegroup extern

    rule extern: s |- ExternRef r : NoNull ExternHT

relation Val_ok: store |- value : valtype

  rulegroup num

    rule num: s |- NumV num : NumT nt
    -- Num_ok: s |- num : nt

  rulegroup vec

    rule vec: s |- VecV vec : VecT vt
    -- Vec_ok: s |- vec : vt

  rulegroup ref

    rule ref: s |- RefV r : RefT rt
    -- Ref_ok: s |- r : rt

def $match_externtype(deftype*, externtype, externtype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, ExtFunct dt_1, ExtFunct dt_2) = $match_deftype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, dt_1, dt_2)

  clause 1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, ExtGlobalT gt_1, ExtGlobalT gt_2) = $match_globaltype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, gt_1, gt_2)

  clause 2 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, ExtTableT tt_1, ExtTableT tt_2) = $match_tabletype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, tt_1, tt_2)

  clause 3 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, ExtMemoryT mt_1, ExtMemoryT mt_2) = $match_memtype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, mt_1, mt_2)

  clause 4 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, ExtTagT jt_1, ExtTagT jt_2) = $match_tagtype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, jt_1, jt_2)

  clause -1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, _xt, _xt') = false

relation Externaddr_ok: store |- externaddr : externtype

  rulegroup func

    rule func: s |- FuncAddr a : ExtFunct s.FUNCS[a].TYPE

  rulegroup global

    rule global: s |- GlobalAddr a : ExtGlobalT s.GLOBALS[a].TYPE

  rulegroup table

    rule table: s |- TableAddr a : ExtTableT s.TABLES[a].TYPE

  rulegroup mem

    rule mem: s |- MemAddr a : ExtMemoryT s.MEMS[a].TYPE

  rulegroup tag

    rule tag: s |- TagAddr a : ExtTagT s.TAGS[a].TYPE

def $subst_all_valtype(valtype, deftype*) : valtype =

  clause 0 : (t, dt*{dt <- dt*}) = $subst_valtype<deftype*>(dt*{dt <- dt*}, $subst_of, t)

def $subst_all_resulttype(resulttype, deftype*) : resulttype =

  clause 0 : (t*{t <- t*}, dt*{dt <- dt*}) = $subst_resulttype<deftype*>(dt*{dt <- dt*}, $subst_of, t*{t <- t*})

def $subst_all_heaptype(heaptype, deftype*) : heaptype =

  clause 0 : (ht, dt*{dt <- dt*}) = $subst_heaptype<deftype*>(dt*{dt <- dt*}, $subst_of, ht)

def $subst_all_reftype(reftype, deftype*) : reftype =

  clause 0 : (rt, dt*{dt <- dt*}) = $subst_reftype<deftype*>(dt*{dt <- dt*}, $subst_of, rt)

def $subst_all_globaltype(globaltype, deftype*) : globaltype =

  clause 0 : (GlobalT mut t, dt*{dt <- dt*}) = GlobalT mut $subst_all_valtype(t, dt*{dt <- dt*})

def $subst_all_tabletype(tabletype, deftype*) : tabletype =

  clause 0 : (TableT at lim rt, dt*{dt <- dt*}) = TableT at lim $subst_all_reftype(rt, dt*{dt <- dt*})

def $subst_all_memtype(memtype, deftype*) : memtype =

  clause 0 : (mt, dt*{dt <- dt*}) = mt

def $subst_all_tagtype(tagtype, deftype*) : tagtype =

  clause 0 : (TagT dt_0, dt*{dt <- dt*}) = TagT $subst_all_deftype(dt_0, dt*{dt <- dt*})

def $subst_all_externtype(externtype, deftype*) : externtype =

  clause 0 : (ExtFunct dt_0, dt*{dt <- dt*}) = ExtFunct $subst_all_deftype(dt_0, dt*{dt <- dt*})

  clause 1 : (ExtTableT tt, dt*{dt <- dt*}) = ExtTableT $subst_all_tabletype(tt, dt*{dt <- dt*})

  clause 2 : (ExtMemoryT mt, dt*{dt <- dt*}) = ExtMemoryT $subst_all_memtype(mt, dt*{dt <- dt*})

  clause 3 : (ExtGlobalT gt, dt*{dt <- dt*}) = ExtGlobalT $subst_all_globaltype(gt, dt*{dt <- dt*})

  clause 4 : (ExtTagT jt, dt*{dt <- dt*}) = ExtTagT $subst_all_tagtype(jt, dt*{dt <- dt*})

def $subst_all_deftype(deftype, deftype*) : deftype =

  clause 0 : (dt_0, dt*{dt <- dt*}) = $subst_deftype<deftype*>(dt*{dt <- dt*}, $subst_of, dt_0)

def $inst_valtype(moduleinst, valtype) : valtype =

  clause 0 : (mm, t) = $subst_all_valtype(t, mm.TYPES)

def $inst_heaptype(moduleinst, heaptype) : heaptype =

  clause 0 : (mm, ht) = $subst_all_heaptype(ht, mm.TYPES)

def $inst_reftype(moduleinst, reftype) : reftype =

  clause 0 : (mm, rt) = $subst_all_reftype(rt, mm.TYPES)

def $inst_globaltype(moduleinst, globaltype) : globaltype =

  clause 0 : (mm, gt) = $subst_all_globaltype(gt, mm.TYPES)

def $inst_tabletype(moduleinst, tabletype) : tabletype =

  clause 0 : (mm, tt) = $subst_all_tabletype(tt, mm.TYPES)

def $inst_memtype(moduleinst, memtype) : memtype =

  clause 0 : (mm, mt) = $subst_all_memtype(mt, mm.TYPES)

def $inst_tagtype(moduleinst, tagtype) : tagtype =

  clause 0 : (mm, jt) = $subst_all_tagtype(jt, mm.TYPES)

def $inst_externtype(moduleinst, externtype) : externtype =

  clause 0 : (mm, xt) = $subst_all_externtype(xt, mm.TYPES)

def $deftype_at(deftype*, typeidx) : deftype =

  clause 0 : (dt_h :: dt_t*{dt_t <- dt_t*}, x) = dt_h
  -- if (x = 0)

  clause 1 : (dt_h :: dt_t*{dt_t <- dt_t*}, x) = $deftype_at(dt_t*{dt_t <- dt_t*}, x')
  -- if (x > 0)
  -- if (x' as int = (x - 1))

def $match_limits(limits, limits) : bool =

  clause 0 : ({MIN i64_1, MAX _fail64?{_fail64 <- _fail64?}}, {MIN i64_2, MAX ?()}) = true
  -- if $le_u(i64_2, i64_1)

  clause 1 : ({MIN i64_1, MAX ?(i64_1')}, {MIN i64_2, MAX ?(i64_2')}) = true
  -- if ($le_u(i64_2, i64_1) /\ $le_u(i64_1', i64_2'))

  clause -1 : (_lim, _lim') = false

def $match_globaltype(deftype*, globaltype, globaltype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, GlobalT Cons t_1, GlobalT Cons t_2) = $match_valtype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, t_1, t_2)

  clause 1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, GlobalT Var t_1, GlobalT Var t_2) = ($match_valtype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, t_1, t_2) /\ $match_valtype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, t_2, t_1))

  clause -1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, _gt, _gt') = false

def $match_tabletype(deftype*, tabletype, tabletype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, TableT at_1 lim_1 rt_1, TableT at_2 lim_2 rt_2) = ((((at_1 = at_2) /\ $match_limits(lim_1, lim_2)) /\ $match_reftype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, rt_1, rt_2)) /\ $match_reftype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, rt_2, rt_1))

def $match_memtype(deftype*, memtype, memtype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, MemoryT at_1 lim_1, MemoryT at_2 lim_2) = ((at_1 = at_2) /\ $match_limits(lim_1, lim_2))

def $match_tagtype(deftype*, tagtype, tagtype) : bool =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, TagT dt_1, TagT dt_2) = ($match_deftype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, dt_1, dt_2) /\ $match_deftype(dt*{dt <- dt*}, dt_2, dt_1))

relation Eval_const: store moduleinst |- const : (store, value)

  rulegroup exec

    rule exec: s mm |- const : (s', v)
    -- Exec_instrs: s mm [] |- const : (s', v :: [])

relation Eval_consts: store moduleinst |- const* : (store, value*)

  rulegroup cons

    rule cons: s mm |- const_1 :: const*{const <- const*} : (s_2, v_1 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- Eval_const: s mm |- const_1 : (s_1, v_1)
    -- Eval_consts: s_1 mm |- const*{const <- const*} : (s_2, v*{v <- v*})

  rulegroup nil

    rule nil: s mm |- [] : (s, [])

relation Exec_instr: store moduleinst value* |- instr : (store, value*)

  rulegroup nop

    rule nop: s mm v*{v <- v*} |- NOP : (s, v*{v <- v*})

  rulegroup drop

    rule drop: s mm v_1 :: v*{v <- v*} |- DROP : (s, v*{v <- v*})

  rulegroup select-true

    rule select-true: s mm NumV I32 i :: v_2 :: v_1 :: v*{v <- v*} |- SELECT t*{t <- t*}?{t* <- t*?} : (s, v_1 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (i =/= 0)

  rulegroup select-false

    rule select-false: s mm NumV I32 i :: v_2 :: v_1 :: v*{v <- v*} |- SELECT t*{t <- t*}?{t* <- t*?} : (s, v_2 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (i = 0)

  rulegroup const

    rule const: s mm v*{v <- v*} |- CONST num : (s, NumV num :: v*{v <- v*})

  rulegroup vec

    rule vec: s mm v*{v <- v*} |- VEC.CONST vec : (s, VecV vec :: v*{v <- v*})

  rulegroup ref-null

    rule ref-null: s mm v*{v <- v*} |- REF.NULL ht : (s, RefV NullRef ht' :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ht' = $inst_heaptype(mm, ht))

  rulegroup ref-func

    rule ref-func: s mm v*{v <- v*} |- REF.FUNC x : (s, RefV FuncRef mm.FUNCS[x] :: v*{v <- v*})

  rulegroup ref-i31

    rule ref-i31: s mm NumV I32 n :: v*{v <- v*} |- REF.I31 : (s, RefV I31Ref ni :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ni = $i31_of_i32(n))

  rulegroup i31-get-u

    rule i31-get-u: s mm RefV I31Ref ni :: v*{v <- v*} |- I31.GET ZX : (s, NumV I32 ni :: v*{v <- v*})

  rulegroup i31-get-s

    rule i31-get-s: s mm RefV I31Ref ni :: v*{v <- v*} |- I31.GET SX : (s, NumV I32 n :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (n = $i31_get_s(ni))

  rulegroup ref-is-null-true

    rule ref-is-null-true: s mm RefV NullRef ht :: v*{v <- v*} |- REF.IS_NULL : (s, NumV I32 1 :: v*{v <- v*})

  rulegroup ref-is-null-false

    rule ref-is-null-false: s mm RefV r :: v*{v <- v*} |- REF.IS_NULL : (s, NumV I32 0 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- Ref_ok: s |- r : NoNull ht

  rulegroup ref-as-non-null

    rule ref-as-non-null: s mm RefV r :: v*{v <- v*} |- REF.AS_NON_NULL : (s, RefV r :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- Ref_ok: s |- r : NoNull ht

  rulegroup ref-eq

    rule ref-eq: s mm RefV r_2 :: RefV r_1 :: v*{v <- v*} |- REF.EQ : (s, NumV I32 n :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (n = $ref_eq(r_1, r_2))

  rulegroup ref-test-true

    rule ref-test-true: s mm RefV r :: v*{v <- v*} |- REF.TEST rt : (s, NumV I32 1 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- Ref_ok: s |- r : rtactual
    -- if (rt_expect = $inst_reftype(mm, rt))
    -- if $match_reftype(mm.TYPES, rtactual, rt_expect)

  rulegroup ref-test-false

    rule ref-test-false: s mm RefV r :: v*{v <- v*} |- REF.TEST rt : (s, NumV I32 0 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- Ref_ok: s |- r : rtactual
    -- if (rt_expect = $inst_reftype(mm, rt))
    -- if ~$match_reftype(mm.TYPES, rtactual, rt_expect)

  rulegroup ref-cast

    rule ref-cast: s mm RefV r :: v*{v <- v*} |- REF.CAST rt : (s, RefV r :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- Ref_ok: s |- r : rtactual
    -- if (rt_expect = $inst_reftype(mm, rt))
    -- if $match_reftype(mm.TYPES, rtactual, rt_expect)

  rulegroup extern-internalize-null

    rule extern-internalize-null: s mm RefV NullRef ht :: v*{v <- v*} |- EXTERN.CONVERT Internalize : (s, RefV NullRef NoneHT :: v*{v <- v*})

  rulegroup extern-internalize

    rule extern-internalize: s mm RefV ExternRef r :: v*{v <- v*} |- EXTERN.CONVERT Internalize : (s, RefV r :: v*{v <- v*})

  rulegroup extern-externalize-null

    rule extern-externalize-null: s mm RefV NullRef ht :: v*{v <- v*} |- EXTERN.CONVERT Externalize : (s, RefV NullRef NoExternHT :: v*{v <- v*})

  rulegroup extern-externalize

    rule extern-externalize: s mm RefV r :: v*{v <- v*} |- EXTERN.CONVERT Externalize : (s, RefV ExternRef r :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- Ref_ok: s |- r : NoNull ht

  rulegroup global-get

    rule global-get: s mm v*{v <- v*} |- GLOBAL.GET x : (s, s.GLOBALS[mm.GLOBALS[x]].VALUE :: v*{v <- v*})

  rulegroup table-get

    rule table-get: s mm NumV I32 i :: v*{v <- v*} |- TABLE.GET x : (s, RefV r :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ta = mm.TABLES[x])
    -- if (TableT I32AT lim rt = s.TABLES[ta].TYPE)
    -- if (r = s.TABLES[ta].REFS[i])

  rulegroup table-get-i64

    rule table-get-i64: s mm NumV I64 i as i64 :: v*{v <- v*} |- TABLE.GET x : (s, RefV r :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ta = mm.TABLES[x])
    -- if (TableT I64AT lim rt = s.TABLES[ta].TYPE)
    -- if (offset = $nat_of_num(I64 i as i64))
    -- if (r = s.TABLES[ta].REFS[offset])

  rulegroup table-set

    rule table-set: s mm RefV r :: NumV num :: v*{v <- v*} |- TABLE.SET x : (s', v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ta = mm.TABLES[x])
    -- if (offset = $nat_of_num(num))
    -- if (offset < |s.TABLES[ta].REFS|)
    -- if (s' = s[TABLES[ta].REFS[offset] = r])

  rulegroup table-size-i32

    rule table-size-i32: s mm v*{v <- v*} |- TABLE.SIZE x : (s, NumV I32 n :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ta = mm.TABLES[x])
    -- if (TableT I32AT lim rt = s.TABLES[ta].TYPE)
    -- if (n = |s.TABLES[ta].REFS|)

  rulegroup table-size-i64

    rule table-size-i64: s mm v*{v <- v*} |- TABLE.SIZE x : (s, NumV I64 n as i64 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ta = mm.TABLES[x])
    -- if (TableT I64AT lim rt = s.TABLES[ta].TYPE)
    -- if (n = |s.TABLES[ta].REFS|)

  rulegroup table-grow-i32-unbounded

    rule table-grow-i32-unbounded: s mm NumV I32 n :: RefV r :: v*{v <- v*} |- TABLE.GROW x : (s', NumV I32 old :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ta = mm.TABLES[x])
    -- if (TableT I32AT {MIN i64, MAX ?()} rt = s.TABLES[ta].TYPE)
    -- if (old = |s.TABLES[ta].REFS|)
    -- if (newsize = (old + n))
    -- if (newsize <= 4294967295)
    -- if (s_1 = s[TABLES[ta].REFS = s.TABLES[ta].REFS ++ $make_<refval>(r, n)])
    -- if (s' = s_1[TABLES[ta].TYPE = TableT I32AT {MIN newsize as i64, MAX ?()} rt])

  rulegroup table-grow-i32-unbounded-fail

    rule table-grow-i32-unbounded-fail: s mm NumV I32 n :: RefV r :: v*{v <- v*} |- TABLE.GROW x : (s, NumV I32 4294967295 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ta = mm.TABLES[x])
    -- if (TableT I32AT {MIN i64, MAX ?()} rt = s.TABLES[ta].TYPE)
    -- if (old = |s.TABLES[ta].REFS|)
    -- if (newsize = (old + n))
    -- if (newsize > 4294967295)

  rulegroup table-grow-i32-bounded

    rule table-grow-i32-bounded: s mm NumV I32 n :: RefV r :: v*{v <- v*} |- TABLE.GROW x : (s', NumV I32 old :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ta = mm.TABLES[x])
    -- if (TableT I32AT {MIN i64, MAX ?(i64_1)} rt = s.TABLES[ta].TYPE)
    -- if (old = |s.TABLES[ta].REFS|)
    -- if (max as i64 = i64_1)
    -- if (newsize = (old + n))
    -- if (newsize <= max)
    -- if (s_1 = s[TABLES[ta].REFS = s.TABLES[ta].REFS ++ $make_<refval>(r, n)])
    -- if (s' = s_1[TABLES[ta].TYPE = TableT I32AT {MIN newsize as i64, MAX ?(i64_1)} rt])

  rulegroup table-grow-i32-fail

    rule table-grow-i32-fail: s mm NumV I32 n :: RefV r :: v*{v <- v*} |- TABLE.GROW x : (s, NumV I32 4294967295 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ta = mm.TABLES[x])
    -- if (TableT I32AT {MIN i64, MAX ?(i64_1)} rt = s.TABLES[ta].TYPE)
    -- if (old = |s.TABLES[ta].REFS|)
    -- if (max as i64 = i64_1)
    -- if (newsize = (old + n))
    -- if (newsize > max)

  rulegroup table-grow-i64-unbounded

    rule table-grow-i64-unbounded: s mm NumV I64 n as i64 :: RefV r :: v*{v <- v*} |- TABLE.GROW x : (s', NumV I64 old as i64 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ta = mm.TABLES[x])
    -- if (TableT I64AT {MIN i64, MAX ?()} rt = s.TABLES[ta].TYPE)
    -- if (old = |s.TABLES[ta].REFS|)
    -- if (nsize = $nat_of_num(I64 n as i64))
    -- if (newsize = (old + nsize))
    -- if (s_1 = s[TABLES[ta].REFS = s.TABLES[ta].REFS ++ $make_<refval>(r, nsize)])
    -- if (s' = s_1[TABLES[ta].TYPE = TableT I64AT {MIN newsize as i64, MAX ?()} rt])

  rulegroup table-grow-i64-bounded

    rule table-grow-i64-bounded: s mm NumV I64 n as i64 :: RefV r :: v*{v <- v*} |- TABLE.GROW x : (s', NumV I64 old as i64 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ta = mm.TABLES[x])
    -- if (TableT I64AT {MIN i64, MAX ?(i64_1)} rt = s.TABLES[ta].TYPE)
    -- if (old = |s.TABLES[ta].REFS|)
    -- if (max as i64 = i64_1)
    -- if (nsize = $nat_of_num(I64 n as i64))
    -- if (newsize = (old + nsize))
    -- if (newsize <= max)
    -- if (s_1 = s[TABLES[ta].REFS = s.TABLES[ta].REFS ++ $make_<refval>(r, nsize)])
    -- if (s' = s_1[TABLES[ta].TYPE = TableT I64AT {MIN newsize as i64, MAX ?(i64_1)} rt])

  rulegroup table-grow-i64-fail

    rule table-grow-i64-fail: s mm NumV I64 n as i64 :: RefV r :: v*{v <- v*} |- TABLE.GROW x : (s, NumV I64 fail64 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ta = mm.TABLES[x])
    -- if (TableT I64AT {MIN i64, MAX ?(i64_1)} rt = s.TABLES[ta].TYPE)
    -- if (old = |s.TABLES[ta].REFS|)
    -- if (max as i64 = i64_1)
    -- if (nsize = $nat_of_num(I64 n as i64))
    -- if (newsize = (old + nsize))
    -- if (newsize > max)
    -- if (fail64 = -1 as i64)

  rulegroup table-fill

    rule table-fill: s mm NumV num_n :: RefV r :: NumV num_i :: v*{v <- v*} |- TABLE.FILL x : (s', v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ta = mm.TABLES[x])
    -- if (offset = $nat_of_num(num_i))
    -- if (n = $nat_of_num(num_n))
    -- if ((offset + n) <= |s.TABLES[ta].REFS|)
    -- if (s' = s[TABLES[ta].REFS = $replace_slice_<refval>(s.TABLES[ta].REFS, offset, $make_<refval>(r, n))])

  rulegroup table-copy

    rule table-copy: s mm NumV num_n :: NumV num_s :: NumV num_d :: v*{v <- v*} |- TABLE.COPY x y : (s', v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ta = mm.TABLES[x])
    -- if (ta' = mm.TABLES[y])
    -- if (n = $nat_of_num(num_n))
    -- if (offset = $nat_of_num(num_d))
    -- if (src = $nat_of_num(num_s))
    -- if ((offset + n) <= |s.TABLES[ta].REFS|)
    -- if ((src + n) <= |s.TABLES[ta'].REFS|)
    -- if (r*{r <- r*} = s.TABLES[ta'].REFS[src : n])
    -- if (s' = s[TABLES[ta].REFS = $replace_slice_<refval>(s.TABLES[ta].REFS, offset, r*{r <- r*})])

  rulegroup table-init

    rule table-init: s mm NumV num_n :: NumV num_s :: NumV num_d :: v*{v <- v*} |- TABLE.INIT x y : (s', v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ta = mm.TABLES[x])
    -- if (ea = mm.ELEMS[y])
    -- if (n = $nat_of_num(num_n))
    -- if (offset = $nat_of_num(num_d))
    -- if (src = $nat_of_num(num_s))
    -- if ((offset + n) <= |s.TABLES[ta].REFS|)
    -- if ((src + n) <= |s.ELEMS[ea].REFS|)
    -- if (r*{r <- r*} = s.ELEMS[ea].REFS[src : n])
    -- if (s' = s[TABLES[ta].REFS = $replace_slice_<refval>(s.TABLES[ta].REFS, offset, r*{r <- r*})])

  rulegroup elem-drop

    rule elem-drop: s mm v*{v <- v*} |- ELEM.DROP x : (s', v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ea = mm.ELEMS[x])
    -- if (s' = s[ELEMS[ea].REFS = []])

  rulegroup data-drop

    rule data-drop: s mm v*{v <- v*} |- DATA.DROP x : (s', v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (da = mm.DATAS[x])
    -- if (s' = s[DATAS[da].BYTES = []])

  rulegroup memory-fill

    rule memory-fill: s mm NumV num_n :: NumV I32 k :: NumV num_i :: v*{v <- v*} |- MEMORY.FILL x : (s', v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ma = mm.MEMS[x])
    -- if (n = $nat_of_num(num_n))
    -- if (offset = $nat_of_num(num_i))
    -- if (byteval = $byte_of_i32(k))
    -- if ((offset + n) <= |s.MEMS[ma].BYTES|)
    -- if (b*{b <- b*} = $repeat_byte(byteval, n))
    -- if (s' = s[MEMS[ma].BYTES = $replace_bytes(s.MEMS[ma].BYTES, offset, b*{b <- b*})])

  rulegroup memory-copy

    rule memory-copy: s mm NumV num_n :: NumV num_s :: NumV num_d :: v*{v <- v*} |- MEMORY.COPY x y : (s', v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ma = mm.MEMS[x])
    -- if (ma' = mm.MEMS[y])
    -- if (n = $nat_of_num(num_n))
    -- if (offset = $nat_of_num(num_d))
    -- if (src = $nat_of_num(num_s))
    -- if ((offset + n) <= |s.MEMS[ma].BYTES|)
    -- if ((src + n) <= |s.MEMS[ma'].BYTES|)
    -- if (b*{b <- b*} = $slice_bytes(s.MEMS[ma'].BYTES, src, n))
    -- if (s' = s[MEMS[ma].BYTES = $replace_bytes(s.MEMS[ma].BYTES, offset, b*{b <- b*})])

  rulegroup memory-init

    rule memory-init: s mm NumV num_n :: NumV num_s :: NumV num_d :: v*{v <- v*} |- MEMORY.INIT x y : (s', v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ma = mm.MEMS[x])
    -- if (da = mm.DATAS[y])
    -- if (n = $nat_of_num(num_n))
    -- if (offset = $nat_of_num(num_d))
    -- if (src = $nat_of_num(num_s))
    -- if ((offset + n) <= |s.MEMS[ma].BYTES|)
    -- if ((src + n) <= |s.DATAS[da].BYTES|)
    -- if (b*{b <- b*} = $slice_bytes(s.DATAS[da].BYTES, src, n))
    -- if (s' = s[MEMS[ma].BYTES = $replace_bytes(s.MEMS[ma].BYTES, offset, b*{b <- b*})])

  rulegroup call

    rule call: s mm v*{v <- v*} |- CALL x : (s', v'*{v' <- v'*})
    -- if (fa = mm.FUNCS[x])
    -- Invoke_funcaddr: s v*{v <- v*} |- fa : (s', v'*{v' <- v'*})

  rulegroup call-ref

    rule call-ref: s mm RefV FuncRef fa :: v*{v <- v*} |- CALL_REF x : (s', v'*{v' <- v'*})
    -- Invoke_funcaddr: s v*{v <- v*} |- fa : (s', v'*{v' <- v'*})

  rulegroup call-indirect

    rule call-indirect: s mm NumV num_i :: v*{v <- v*} |- CALL_INDIRECT x y : (s', v'*{v' <- v'*})
    -- if (ta = mm.TABLES[x])
    -- if (i = $nat_of_num(num_i))
    -- if (FuncRef fa = s.TABLES[ta].REFS[i])
    -- if (fi = s.FUNCS[fa])
    -- if $match_deftype([], fi.TYPE, mm.TYPES[y])
    -- Invoke_funcaddr: s v*{v <- v*} |- fa : (s', v'*{v' <- v'*})

  rulegroup binop-i32-add

    -- if (src = $nat_of_num(num_s))
    -- if (offset = $nat_of_num(num_i))
    -- if (DefArrayT ArrayT FieldT mut zt = $expanddt(s.ARRAYS[a].TYPE))
    -- if ((src + n) <= |s.ELEMS[ea].REFS|)
    -- if ((offset + n) <= |s.ARRAYS[a].FIELDS|)
    -- if (r*{r <- r*} = s.ELEMS[ea].REFS[src : n])
    -- if (fv*{fv <- fv*} = $fieldvals_of_refs(zt, r*{r <- r*}))
    -- if (s' = s[ARRAYS[a].FIELDS = $replace_slice_<fieldval>(s.ARRAYS[a].FIELDS, offset, fv*{fv <- fv*})])

  rulegroup load-num-generic

    rule load-num-generic: s mm NumV num_1 :: v*{v <- v*} |- LOAD x lop : (s, NumV num_2 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ma = mm.MEMS[x])
    -- if (addrval = $addr_plus_offset(num_1, lop.OFFSET))
    -- if (nsize = $loadop_size(lop))
    -- if ((addrval + nsize) <= |s.MEMS[ma].BYTES|)
    -- if (b*{b <- b*} = s.MEMS[ma].BYTES[addrval : nsize])
    -- if (num_2 = $eval_load_num(lop, b*{b <- b*}))

  rulegroup store-num-generic

    rule store-num-generic: s mm NumV num_2 :: NumV num_1 :: v*{v <- v*} |- STORE x sop : (s', v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ma = mm.MEMS[x])
    -- if (addrval = $addr_plus_offset(num_1, sop.OFFSET))
    -- if (nsize = $storeop_size(sop))
    -- if ((addrval + nsize) <= |s.MEMS[ma].BYTES|)
    -- if (b*{b <- b*} = $eval_store_num(sop, num_2))
    -- if (s' = s[MEMS[ma].BYTES = $replace_bytes(s.MEMS[ma].BYTES, addrval, b*{b <- b*})])

  rulegroup load-vec-generic

    rule load-vec-generic: s mm NumV num :: v*{v <- v*} |- VEC.LOAD x vlop : (s, VecV vec :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ma = mm.MEMS[x])
    -- if (addrval = $addr_plus_offset(num, vlop.OFFSET))
    -- if (nsize = $vloadop_size(vlop))
    -- if ((addrval + nsize) <= |s.MEMS[ma].BYTES|)
    -- if (b*{b <- b*} = s.MEMS[ma].BYTES[addrval : nsize])
    -- if (vec = $eval_load_vec(vlop, b*{b <- b*}))

  rulegroup store-vec-generic

    rule store-vec-generic: s mm VecV vec :: NumV num :: v*{v <- v*} |- VEC.STORE x vsop : (s', v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ma = mm.MEMS[x])
    -- if (addrval = $addr_plus_offset(num, vsop.OFFSET))
    -- if (nsize = $vstoreop_size(vsop))
    -- if ((addrval + nsize) <= |s.MEMS[ma].BYTES|)
    -- if (b*{b <- b*} = $eval_store_vec(vsop, vec))
    -- if (s' = s[MEMS[ma].BYTES = $replace_bytes(s.MEMS[ma].BYTES, addrval, b*{b <- b*})])

  rulegroup load-vec-lane-generic

    rule load-vec-lane-generic: s mm VecV vec_1 :: NumV num :: v*{v <- v*} |- VEC.LOAD_LANE x vlaneop int_1 : (s, VecV vec_2 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ma = mm.MEMS[x])
    -- if (addrval = $addr_plus_offset(num, vlaneop.OFFSET))
    -- if (nsize = $vlaneop_size(vlaneop))
    -- if ((addrval + nsize) <= |s.MEMS[ma].BYTES|)
    -- if (b*{b <- b*} = s.MEMS[ma].BYTES[addrval : nsize])
    -- if (vec_2 = $eval_load_vec_lane(vlaneop, int_1, b*{b <- b*}, vec_1))

  rulegroup store-vec-lane-generic

    rule store-vec-lane-generic: s mm VecV vec :: NumV num :: v*{v <- v*} |- VEC.STORE_LANE x vlaneop int_1 : (s', v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ma = mm.MEMS[x])
    -- if (addrval = $addr_plus_offset(num, vlaneop.OFFSET))
    -- if (nsize = $vlaneop_size(vlaneop))
    -- if ((addrval + nsize) <= |s.MEMS[ma].BYTES|)
    -- if (b*{b <- b*} = $eval_store_vec_lane(vlaneop, int_1, vec))
    -- if (s' = s[MEMS[ma].BYTES = $replace_bytes(s.MEMS[ma].BYTES, addrval, b*{b <- b*})])

relation Exec_instrs: store moduleinst value* |- instr* : (store, value*)

  rulegroup cons

    rule cons: s mm v*{v <- v*} |- instr_1 :: instr*{instr <- instr*} : (s_2, v_2*{v_2 <- v_2*})
    -- Exec_instr: s mm v*{v <- v*} |- instr_1 : (s_1, v_1*{v_1 <- v_1*})
    -- Exec_instrs: s_1 mm v_1*{v_1 <- v_1*} |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : (s_2, v_2*{v_2 <- v_2*})

  rulegroup nil

    rule nil: s mm v*{v <- v*} |- [] : (s, v*{v <- v*})

relation Exec_instr_frame: store frame value* |- instr : (store, frame, value*)

  rulegroup base

    rule base: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- instr : (s', fr, v'*{v' <- v'*})
    -- Exec_instr: s fr.MODULE v*{v <- v*} |- instr : (s', v'*{v' <- v'*})

  rulegroup local-get

    rule local-get: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- LOCAL.GET x : (s, fr, v_1 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (?(v_1) = fr.LOCALS[x])

  rulegroup local-set

    rule local-set: s fr v_1 :: v*{v <- v*} |- LOCAL.SET x : (s, fr', v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (fr' = fr[LOCALS[x] = ?(v_1)])

  rulegroup local-tee

    rule local-tee: s fr v_1 :: v*{v <- v*} |- LOCAL.TEE x : (s, fr', v_1 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (fr' = fr[LOCALS[x] = ?(v_1)])

  rulegroup global-set

    rule global-set: s fr v_1 :: v*{v <- v*} |- GLOBAL.SET x : (s', fr, v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ga = fr.MODULE.GLOBALS[x])
    -- if (s' = s[GLOBALS[ga].VALUE = v_1])

  rulegroup memory-size-i32

    rule memory-size-i32: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- MEMORY.SIZE x : (s, fr, NumV I32 pages :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ma = fr.MODULE.MEMS[x])
    -- if (MemoryT I32AT lim = s.MEMS[ma].TYPE)
    -- if (pages = (|s.MEMS[ma].BYTES| / $page_size))

  rulegroup memory-size-i64

    rule memory-size-i64: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- MEMORY.SIZE x : (s, fr, NumV I64 pages as i64 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ma = fr.MODULE.MEMS[x])
    -- if (MemoryT I64AT lim = s.MEMS[ma].TYPE)
    -- if (pages = (|s.MEMS[ma].BYTES| / $page_size))

  rulegroup memory-grow-i32

    rule memory-grow-i32: s fr NumV I32 n :: v*{v <- v*} |- MEMORY.GROW x : (s', fr, NumV I32 pages :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ma = fr.MODULE.MEMS[x])
    -- if (MemoryT I32AT {MIN i64, MAX ?()} = s.MEMS[ma].TYPE)
    -- if (pages = (|s.MEMS[ma].BYTES| / $page_size))
    -- if (newsize = (pages + n))
    -- if (newsize <= 65536)
    -- if (nbytes = (n * $page_size))
    -- if (s_1 = s[MEMS[ma].BYTES = s.MEMS[ma].BYTES ++ $zero_bytes(nbytes)])
    -- if (s' = s_1[MEMS[ma].TYPE = MemoryT I32AT {MIN newsize as i64, MAX ?()}])

  rulegroup memory-grow-i32-unbounded-fail

    rule memory-grow-i32-unbounded-fail: s fr NumV I32 n :: v*{v <- v*} |- MEMORY.GROW x : (s, fr, NumV I32 4294967295 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ma = fr.MODULE.MEMS[x])
    -- if (MemoryT I32AT {MIN i64, MAX ?()} = s.MEMS[ma].TYPE)
    -- if (pages = (|s.MEMS[ma].BYTES| / $page_size))
    -- if (newsize = (pages + n))
    -- if (newsize > 65536)

  rulegroup memory-grow-i32-bounded

    rule memory-grow-i32-bounded: s fr NumV I32 n :: v*{v <- v*} |- MEMORY.GROW x : (s', fr, NumV I32 pages :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ma = fr.MODULE.MEMS[x])
    -- if (MemoryT I32AT {MIN i64, MAX ?(i64_1)} = s.MEMS[ma].TYPE)
    -- if (max as i64 = i64_1)
    -- if (pages = (|s.MEMS[ma].BYTES| / $page_size))
    -- if (newsize = (pages + n))
    -- if (newsize <= 65536)
    -- if (newsize <= max)
    -- if (nbytes = (n * $page_size))
    -- if (s_1 = s[MEMS[ma].BYTES = s.MEMS[ma].BYTES ++ $zero_bytes(nbytes)])
    -- if (s' = s_1[MEMS[ma].TYPE = MemoryT I32AT {MIN newsize as i64, MAX ?(i64_1)}])

  rulegroup memory-grow-i32-bounded-max-fail

    rule memory-grow-i32-bounded-max-fail: s fr NumV I32 n :: v*{v <- v*} |- MEMORY.GROW x : (s, fr, NumV I32 4294967295 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ma = fr.MODULE.MEMS[x])
    -- if (MemoryT I32AT {MIN i64, MAX ?(i64_1)} = s.MEMS[ma].TYPE)
    -- if (max as i64 = i64_1)
    -- if (pages = (|s.MEMS[ma].BYTES| / $page_size))
    -- if (newsize = (pages + n))
    -- if (newsize > max)

  rulegroup memory-grow-i32-bounded-size-fail

    rule memory-grow-i32-bounded-size-fail: s fr NumV I32 n :: v*{v <- v*} |- MEMORY.GROW x : (s, fr, NumV I32 4294967295 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ma = fr.MODULE.MEMS[x])
    -- if (MemoryT I32AT {MIN i64, MAX ?(i64_1)} = s.MEMS[ma].TYPE)
    -- if (pages = (|s.MEMS[ma].BYTES| / $page_size))
    -- if (newsize = (pages + n))
    -- if (newsize > 65536)

  rulegroup memory-grow-i64

    rule memory-grow-i64: s fr NumV I64 n as i64 :: v*{v <- v*} |- MEMORY.GROW x : (s', fr, NumV I64 pages as i64 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ma = fr.MODULE.MEMS[x])
    -- if (MemoryT I64AT {MIN i64, MAX ?()} = s.MEMS[ma].TYPE)
    -- if (pages = (|s.MEMS[ma].BYTES| / $page_size))
    -- if (nsize = $nat_of_num(I64 n as i64))
    -- if (newsize = (pages + nsize))
    -- if (nbytes = (nsize * $page_size))
    -- if (s_1 = s[MEMS[ma].BYTES = s.MEMS[ma].BYTES ++ $zero_bytes(nbytes)])
    -- if (s' = s_1[MEMS[ma].TYPE = MemoryT I64AT {MIN newsize as i64, MAX ?()}])

  rulegroup memory-grow-i64-bounded

    rule memory-grow-i64-bounded: s fr NumV I64 n as i64 :: v*{v <- v*} |- MEMORY.GROW x : (s', fr, NumV I64 pages as i64 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ma = fr.MODULE.MEMS[x])
    -- if (MemoryT I64AT {MIN i64, MAX ?(i64_1)} = s.MEMS[ma].TYPE)
    -- if (max as i64 = i64_1)
    -- if (pages = (|s.MEMS[ma].BYTES| / $page_size))
    -- if (nsize = $nat_of_num(I64 n as i64))
    -- if (newsize = (pages + nsize))
    -- if (newsize <= max)
    -- if (nbytes = (nsize * $page_size))
    -- if (s_1 = s[MEMS[ma].BYTES = s.MEMS[ma].BYTES ++ $zero_bytes(nbytes)])
    -- if (s' = s_1[MEMS[ma].TYPE = MemoryT I64AT {MIN newsize as i64, MAX ?(i64_1)}])

  rulegroup memory-grow-i64-bounded-fail

    rule memory-grow-i64-bounded-fail: s fr NumV I64 n as i64 :: v*{v <- v*} |- MEMORY.GROW x : (s, fr, NumV I64 fail64 :: v*{v <- v*})
    -- if (ma = fr.MODULE.MEMS[x])
    -- if (MemoryT I64AT {MIN i64, MAX ?(i64_1)} = s.MEMS[ma].TYPE)
    -- if (max as i64 = i64_1)
    -- if (pages = (|s.MEMS[ma].BYTES| / $page_size))
    -- if (nsize = $nat_of_num(I64 n as i64))
    -- if (newsize = (pages + nsize))
    -- if (newsize > max)
    -- if (fail64 = -1 as i64)

relation Exec_instrs_frame: store frame value* |- instr* : (store, frame, value*)

  rulegroup cons

    rule cons: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- instr_1 :: instr*{instr <- instr*} : (s_2, fr_2, v_2*{v_2 <- v_2*})
    -- Exec_instr_frame: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- instr_1 : (s_1, fr_1, v_1*{v_1 <- v_1*})
    -- Exec_instrs_frame: s_1 fr_1 v_1*{v_1 <- v_1*} |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : (s_2, fr_2, v_2*{v_2 <- v_2*})

  rulegroup nil

    rule nil: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- [] : (s, fr, v*{v <- v*})

relation Exec_instr_ctrl: store frame value* |- instr : execresult

  rulegroup block-normal

    rule block-normal: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- BLOCK blocktype instr*{instr <- instr*} : NormalR s' frp v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- if (v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} = $block_outer_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : NormalR s' frp v_label*{v_label <- v_label*}
    -- if (v'*{v' <- v'*} = v_label*{v_label <- v_label*} ++ v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

  rulegroup block-break-zero

    rule block-break-zero: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- BLOCK blocktype instr*{instr <- instr*} : NormalR s' frp v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (nresult = $block_result_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- if (v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} = $block_outer_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : BreakR s' frp 0 v_break*{v_break <- v_break*}
    -- if (v_take*{v_take <- v_take*} = $take_<value>(v_break*{v_break <- v_break*}, nresult))
    -- if (v'*{v' <- v'*} = v_take*{v_take <- v_take*} ++ v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

  rulegroup block-break-succ

    rule block-break-succ: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- BLOCK blocktype instr*{instr <- instr*} : BreakR s' frp l' v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : BreakR s' frp l v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (l > 0)
    -- if (l' as int = (l - 1))

  rulegroup block-return

    rule block-return: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- BLOCK blocktype instr*{instr <- instr*} : ReturnR s' frp v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : ReturnR s' frp v'*{v' <- v'*}

  rulegroup block-throw

    rule block-throw: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- BLOCK blocktype instr*{instr <- instr*} : ThrowR s' frp ja v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : ThrowR s' frp ja v'*{v' <- v'*}

  rulegroup loop-normal

    rule loop-normal: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- LOOP blocktype instr*{instr <- instr*} : NormalR s' frp v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- if (v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} = $block_outer_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : NormalR s' frp v_label*{v_label <- v_label*}
    -- if (v'*{v' <- v'*} = v_label*{v_label <- v_label*} ++ v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

  rulegroup loop-break-zero

    rule loop-break-zero: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- LOOP blocktype instr*{instr <- instr*} : er
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- if (v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} = $block_outer_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : BreakR s_1 fr_1 0 v_1*{v_1 <- v_1*}
    -- if (v_loop*{v_loop <- v_loop*} = $take_<value>(v_1*{v_1 <- v_1*}, nparam) ++ v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*})
    -- Exec_instr_ctrl: s_1 fr_1 v_loop*{v_loop <- v_loop*} |- LOOP blocktype instr*{instr <- instr*} : er

  rulegroup loop-break-succ

    rule loop-break-succ: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- LOOP blocktype instr*{instr <- instr*} : BreakR s' frp l' v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : BreakR s' frp l v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (l > 0)
    -- if (l' as int = (l - 1))

  rulegroup loop-return

    rule loop-return: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- LOOP blocktype instr*{instr <- instr*} : ReturnR s' frp v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : ReturnR s' frp v'*{v' <- v'*}

  rulegroup loop-throw

    rule loop-throw: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- LOOP blocktype instr*{instr <- instr*} : ThrowR s' frp ja v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : ThrowR s' frp ja v'*{v' <- v'*}

  rulegroup cond-true-normal

    rule cond-true-normal: s fr NumV I32 i :: v*{v <- v*} |- IF blocktype instr_1*{instr_1 <- instr_1*} ELSE instr_2*{instr_2 <- instr_2*} : NormalR s' frp v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (i =/= 0)
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- if (v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} = $block_outer_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr_1*{instr_1 <- instr_1*} : NormalR s' frp v_label*{v_label <- v_label*}
    -- if (v'*{v' <- v'*} = v_label*{v_label <- v_label*} ++ v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

  rulegroup cond-true-break-zero

    rule cond-true-break-zero: s fr NumV I32 i :: v*{v <- v*} |- IF blocktype instr_1*{instr_1 <- instr_1*} ELSE instr_2*{instr_2 <- instr_2*} : NormalR s' frp v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (i =/= 0)
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (nresult = $block_result_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- if (v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} = $block_outer_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr_1*{instr_1 <- instr_1*} : BreakR s' frp 0 v_break*{v_break <- v_break*}
    -- if (v_take*{v_take <- v_take*} = $take_<value>(v_break*{v_break <- v_break*}, nresult))
    -- if (v'*{v' <- v'*} = v_take*{v_take <- v_take*} ++ v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

  rulegroup cond-true-break-succ

    rule cond-true-break-succ: s fr NumV I32 i :: v*{v <- v*} |- IF blocktype instr_1*{instr_1 <- instr_1*} ELSE instr_2*{instr_2 <- instr_2*} : BreakR s' frp l' v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (i =/= 0)
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr_1*{instr_1 <- instr_1*} : BreakR s' frp l v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (l > 0)
    -- if (l' as int = (l - 1))

  rulegroup cond-true-return

    rule cond-true-return: s fr NumV I32 i :: v*{v <- v*} |- IF blocktype instr_1*{instr_1 <- instr_1*} ELSE instr_2*{instr_2 <- instr_2*} : ReturnR s' frp v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (i =/= 0)
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr_1*{instr_1 <- instr_1*} : ReturnR s' frp v'*{v' <- v'*}

  rulegroup cond-true-throw

    rule cond-true-throw: s fr NumV I32 i :: v*{v <- v*} |- IF blocktype instr_1*{instr_1 <- instr_1*} ELSE instr_2*{instr_2 <- instr_2*} : ThrowR s' frp ja v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (i =/= 0)
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr_1*{instr_1 <- instr_1*} : ThrowR s' frp ja v'*{v' <- v'*}

  rulegroup cond-false-normal

    rule cond-false-normal: s fr NumV I32 i :: v*{v <- v*} |- IF blocktype instr_1*{instr_1 <- instr_1*} ELSE instr_2*{instr_2 <- instr_2*} : NormalR s' frp v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (i = 0)
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- if (v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} = $block_outer_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr_2*{instr_2 <- instr_2*} : NormalR s' frp v_label*{v_label <- v_label*}
    -- if (v'*{v' <- v'*} = v_label*{v_label <- v_label*} ++ v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

  rulegroup cond-false-break-zero

    rule cond-false-break-zero: s fr NumV I32 i :: v*{v <- v*} |- IF blocktype instr_1*{instr_1 <- instr_1*} ELSE instr_2*{instr_2 <- instr_2*} : NormalR s' frp v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (i = 0)
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (nresult = $block_result_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- if (v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} = $block_outer_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr_2*{instr_2 <- instr_2*} : BreakR s' frp 0 v_break*{v_break <- v_break*}
    -- if (v_take*{v_take <- v_take*} = $take_<value>(v_break*{v_break <- v_break*}, nresult))
    -- if (v'*{v' <- v'*} = v_take*{v_take <- v_take*} ++ v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

  rulegroup cond-false-break-succ

    rule cond-false-break-succ: s fr NumV I32 i :: v*{v <- v*} |- IF blocktype instr_1*{instr_1 <- instr_1*} ELSE instr_2*{instr_2 <- instr_2*} : BreakR s' frp l' v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (i = 0)
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr_2*{instr_2 <- instr_2*} : BreakR s' frp l v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (l > 0)
    -- if (l' as int = (l - 1))

  rulegroup cond-false-return

    rule cond-false-return: s fr NumV I32 i :: v*{v <- v*} |- IF blocktype instr_1*{instr_1 <- instr_1*} ELSE instr_2*{instr_2 <- instr_2*} : ReturnR s' frp v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (i = 0)
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr_2*{instr_2 <- instr_2*} : ReturnR s' frp v'*{v' <- v'*}

  rulegroup cond-false-throw

    rule cond-false-throw: s fr NumV I32 i :: v*{v <- v*} |- IF blocktype instr_1*{instr_1 <- instr_1*} ELSE instr_2*{instr_2 <- instr_2*} : ThrowR s' frp ja v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (i = 0)
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr_2*{instr_2 <- instr_2*} : ThrowR s' frp ja v'*{v' <- v'*}

  rulegroup br

    rule br: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- BR l : BreakR s fr l v*{v <- v*}

  rulegroup brif-true

    rule brif-true: s fr NumV I32 i :: v*{v <- v*} |- BR_IF l : BreakR s fr l v*{v <- v*}
    -- if (i =/= 0)

  rulegroup brif-false

    rule brif-false: s fr NumV I32 i :: v*{v <- v*} |- BR_IF l : NormalR s fr v*{v <- v*}
    -- if (i = 0)

  rulegroup br-table-in

    rule br-table-in: s fr NumV I32 i :: v*{v <- v*} |- BR_TABLE l*{l <- l*} x : BreakR s fr y v*{v <- v*}
    -- if (i < |l*{l <- l*}|)
    -- if (y = l*{l <- l*}[i])

  rulegroup br-table-out

    rule br-table-out: s fr NumV I32 i :: v*{v <- v*} |- BR_TABLE l*{l <- l*} x : BreakR s fr x v*{v <- v*}
    -- if (i >= |l*{l <- l*}|)

  rulegroup br-on-null-null

    rule br-on-null-null: s fr RefV NullRef ht :: v*{v <- v*} |- BR_ON_NULL l : BreakR s fr l v*{v <- v*}

  rulegroup br-on-null-non-null

    rule br-on-null-non-null: s fr RefV r :: v*{v <- v*} |- BR_ON_NULL l : NormalR s fr RefV r :: v*{v <- v*}
    -- Ref_ok: s |- r : NoNull ht

  rulegroup br-on-non-null-null

    rule br-on-non-null-null: s fr RefV NullRef ht :: v*{v <- v*} |- BR_ON_NON_NULL l : NormalR s fr v*{v <- v*}

  rulegroup br-on-non-null-non-null

    rule br-on-non-null-non-null: s fr RefV r :: v*{v <- v*} |- BR_ON_NON_NULL l : BreakR s fr l RefV r :: v*{v <- v*}
    -- Ref_ok: s |- r : NoNull ht

  rulegroup br-on-cast-true

    rule br-on-cast-true: s fr RefV r :: v*{v <- v*} |- BR_ON_CAST l rt_1 rt_2 : BreakR s fr l RefV r :: v*{v <- v*}
    -- Ref_ok: s |- r : rtactual
    -- if (rt_expect = $inst_reftype(fr.MODULE, rt_2))
    -- if $match_reftype(fr.MODULE.TYPES, rtactual, rt_expect)

  rulegroup br-on-cast-false

    rule br-on-cast-false: s fr RefV r :: v*{v <- v*} |- BR_ON_CAST l rt_1 rt_2 : NormalR s fr RefV r :: v*{v <- v*}
    -- Ref_ok: s |- r : rtactual
    -- if (rt_expect = $inst_reftype(fr.MODULE, rt_2))
    -- if ~$match_reftype(fr.MODULE.TYPES, rtactual, rt_expect)

  rulegroup br-on-cast-fail-true

    rule br-on-cast-fail-true: s fr RefV r :: v*{v <- v*} |- BR_ON_CAST_FAIL l rt_1 rt_2 : NormalR s fr RefV r :: v*{v <- v*}
    -- Ref_ok: s |- r : rtactual
    -- if (rt_expect = $inst_reftype(fr.MODULE, rt_2))
    -- if $match_reftype(fr.MODULE.TYPES, rtactual, rt_expect)

  rulegroup br-on-cast-fail-false

    rule br-on-cast-fail-false: s fr RefV r :: v*{v <- v*} |- BR_ON_CAST_FAIL l rt_1 rt_2 : BreakR s fr l RefV r :: v*{v <- v*}
    -- Ref_ok: s |- r : rtactual
    -- if (rt_expect = $inst_reftype(fr.MODULE, rt_2))
    -- if ~$match_reftype(fr.MODULE.TYPES, rtactual, rt_expect)

  rulegroup return

    rule return: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- RETURN : ReturnR s fr v*{v <- v*}

  rulegroup return-call

    rule return-call: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- RETURN_CALL x : ReturnR s' fr v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (fa = fr.MODULE.FUNCS[x])
    -- Invoke_funcaddr: s v*{v <- v*} |- fa : (s', v'*{v' <- v'*})

  rulegroup return-call-throw

    rule return-call-throw: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- RETURN_CALL x : ThrowR s' frp ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*}
    -- if (fa = fr.MODULE.FUNCS[x])
    -- Invoke_funcaddr_throw: s v*{v <- v*} |- fa : ThrowR s' frp ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*}

  rulegroup return-call-ref

    rule return-call-ref: s fr RefV FuncRef fa :: v*{v <- v*} |- RETURN_CALL_REF x : ReturnR s' fr v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- Invoke_funcaddr: s v*{v <- v*} |- fa : (s', v'*{v' <- v'*})

  rulegroup return-call-ref-throw

    rule return-call-ref-throw: s fr RefV FuncRef fa :: v*{v <- v*} |- RETURN_CALL_REF x : ThrowR s' frp ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*}
    -- Invoke_funcaddr_throw: s v*{v <- v*} |- fa : ThrowR s' frp ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*}

  rulegroup return-call-indirect

    rule return-call-indirect: s fr NumV num_i :: v*{v <- v*} |- RETURN_CALL_INDIRECT x y : ReturnR s' fr v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (ta = fr.MODULE.TABLES[x])
    -- if (i = $nat_of_num(num_i))
    -- if (FuncRef fa = s.TABLES[ta].REFS[i])
    -- if (fi = s.FUNCS[fa])
    -- if $match_deftype([], fi.TYPE, fr.MODULE.TYPES[y])
    -- Invoke_funcaddr: s v*{v <- v*} |- fa : (s', v'*{v' <- v'*})

  rulegroup return-call-indirect-throw

    rule return-call-indirect-throw: s fr NumV num_i :: v*{v <- v*} |- RETURN_CALL_INDIRECT x y : ThrowR s' frp ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*}
    -- if (ta = fr.MODULE.TABLES[x])
    -- if (i = $nat_of_num(num_i))
    -- if (FuncRef fa = s.TABLES[ta].REFS[i])
    -- if (fi = s.FUNCS[fa])
    -- if $match_deftype([], fi.TYPE, fr.MODULE.TYPES[y])
    -- Invoke_funcaddr_throw: s v*{v <- v*} |- fa : ThrowR s' frp ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*}

  rulegroup call-throw

    rule call-throw: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- CALL x : ThrowR s' frp ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*}
    -- if (fa = fr.MODULE.FUNCS[x])
    -- Invoke_funcaddr_throw: s v*{v <- v*} |- fa : ThrowR s' frp ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*}

  rulegroup call-ref-throw

    rule call-ref-throw: s fr RefV FuncRef fa :: v*{v <- v*} |- CALL_REF x : ThrowR s' frp ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*}
    -- Invoke_funcaddr_throw: s v*{v <- v*} |- fa : ThrowR s' frp ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*}

  rulegroup call-indirect-throw

    rule call-indirect-throw: s fr NumV num_i :: v*{v <- v*} |- CALL_INDIRECT x y : ThrowR s' frp ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*}
    -- if (ta = fr.MODULE.TABLES[x])
    -- if (i = $nat_of_num(num_i))
    -- if (FuncRef fa = s.TABLES[ta].REFS[i])
    -- if (fi = s.FUNCS[fa])
    -- if $match_deftype([], fi.TYPE, fr.MODULE.TYPES[y])
    -- Invoke_funcaddr_throw: s v*{v <- v*} |- fa : ThrowR s' frp ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*}

  rulegroup throw

    rule throw: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- THROW x : ThrowR s fr ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*}
    -- if (ja = fr.MODULE.TAGS[x])
    -- if (TagT dtf = s.TAGS[ja].TYPE)
    -- if (DefFuncT FuncT t_arg*{t_arg <- t_arg*} [] = $expanddt(dtf))
    -- if (n = |t_arg*{t_arg <- t_arg*}|)
    -- if (v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*} = $take_<value>(v*{v <- v*}, n))

  rulegroup throw-ref

    rule throw-ref: s fr RefV ExnRef a :: v*{v <- v*} |- THROW_REF : ThrowR s fr s.EXNS[a].TAG s.EXNS[a].FIELDS

  rulegroup try-table-normal

    rule try-table-normal: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- TRY_TABLE blocktype catch*{catch <- catch*} instr*{instr <- instr*} : NormalR s' frp v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- if (v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} = $block_outer_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : NormalR s' frp v_label*{v_label <- v_label*}
    -- if (v'*{v' <- v'*} = v_label*{v_label <- v_label*} ++ v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

  rulegroup try-table-break-zero

    rule try-table-break-zero: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- TRY_TABLE blocktype catch*{catch <- catch*} instr*{instr <- instr*} : NormalR s' frp v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (nresult = $block_result_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- if (v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} = $block_outer_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : BreakR s' frp 0 v_break*{v_break <- v_break*}
    -- if (v_take*{v_take <- v_take*} = $take_<value>(v_break*{v_break <- v_break*}, nresult))
    -- if (v'*{v' <- v'*} = v_take*{v_take <- v_take*} ++ v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

  rulegroup try-table-break-succ

    rule try-table-break-succ: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- TRY_TABLE blocktype catch*{catch <- catch*} instr*{instr <- instr*} : BreakR s' frp l' v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : BreakR s' frp l v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (l > 0)
    -- if (l' as int = (l - 1))

  rulegroup try-table-return

    rule try-table-return: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- TRY_TABLE blocktype catch*{catch <- catch*} instr*{instr <- instr*} : ReturnR s' frp v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : ReturnR s' frp v'*{v' <- v'*}

  rulegroup try-table-throw

    rule try-table-throw: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- TRY_TABLE blocktype catch*{catch <- catch*} instr*{instr <- instr*} : er
    -- if (nparam = $block_param_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $block_input_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- if (v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} = $block_outer_stack(v*{v <- v*}, nparam))
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : ThrowR s' frp ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*}
    -- Handle_throw: s' frp v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*} |- catch*{catch <- catch*} : er

  rulegroup plain

    rule plain: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- instr : NormalR s' frp v'*{v' <- v'*}
    -- Exec_instr_frame: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- instr : (s', frp, v'*{v' <- v'*})

relation Exec_instrs_ctrl: store frame value* |- instr* : execresult

  rulegroup cons-normal

    rule cons-normal: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- instr_1 :: instr*{instr <- instr*} : er
    -- Exec_instr_ctrl: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- instr_1 : NormalR s_1 fr_1 v_1*{v_1 <- v_1*}
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s_1 fr_1 v_1*{v_1 <- v_1*} |- instr*{instr <- instr*} : er

  rulegroup cons-break

    rule cons-break: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- instr_1 :: instr*{instr <- instr*} : BreakR s_1 fr_1 l v_1*{v_1 <- v_1*}
    -- Exec_instr_ctrl: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- instr_1 : BreakR s_1 fr_1 l v_1*{v_1 <- v_1*}

  rulegroup cons-return

    rule cons-return: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- instr_1 :: instr*{instr <- instr*} : ReturnR s_1 fr_1 v_1*{v_1 <- v_1*}
    -- Exec_instr_ctrl: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- instr_1 : ReturnR s_1 fr_1 v_1*{v_1 <- v_1*}

  rulegroup cons-throw

    rule cons-throw: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- instr_1 :: instr*{instr <- instr*} : ThrowR s_1 fr_1 ja v_1*{v_1 <- v_1*}
    -- Exec_instr_ctrl: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- instr_1 : ThrowR s_1 fr_1 ja v_1*{v_1 <- v_1*}

  rulegroup nil

    rule nil: s fr v*{v <- v*} |- [] : NormalR s fr v*{v <- v*}

relation Invoke_funcaddr: store value* |- funcaddr : (store, value*)

  rulegroup ast

    rule ast: s v*{v <- v*} |- fa : (s', v'*{v' <- v'*})
    -- if (fi = s.FUNCS[fa])
    -- if (AstFunc func = fi.CODE)
    -- if (DefFuncT FuncT t_arg*{t_arg <- t_arg*} t_result*{t_result <- t_result*} = $expanddt(fi.TYPE))
    -- if (n = |t_arg*{t_arg <- t_arg*}|)
    -- if (v_arg_stack*{v_arg_stack <- v_arg_stack*} = $take_<value>(v*{v <- v*}, n))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $rev_<value>(v_arg_stack*{v_arg_stack <- v_arg_stack*}))
    -- if (nrest as int = (|v*{v <- v*}| - n))
    -- if (v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} = v*{v <- v*}[n : nrest])
    -- if (fr = {LOCALS $some_values(v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*}) ++ $default_locals(fi.MODULE, func.LOCALS), MODULE fi.MODULE})
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr [] |- func.BODY : NormalR s' frp v_result_stack*{v_result_stack <- v_result_stack*}
    -- if (m = |t_result*{t_result <- t_result*}|)
    -- if (v_results*{v_results <- v_results*} = $take_<value>(v_result_stack*{v_result_stack <- v_result_stack*}, m))
    -- if (v'*{v' <- v'*} = v_results*{v_results <- v_results*} ++ v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

  rulegroup ast-return

    rule ast-return: s v*{v <- v*} |- fa : (s', v'*{v' <- v'*})
    -- if (fi = s.FUNCS[fa])
    -- if (AstFunc func = fi.CODE)
    -- if (DefFuncT FuncT t_arg*{t_arg <- t_arg*} t_result*{t_result <- t_result*} = $expanddt(fi.TYPE))
    -- if (n = |t_arg*{t_arg <- t_arg*}|)
    -- if (v_arg_stack*{v_arg_stack <- v_arg_stack*} = $take_<value>(v*{v <- v*}, n))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $rev_<value>(v_arg_stack*{v_arg_stack <- v_arg_stack*}))
    -- if (nrest as int = (|v*{v <- v*}| - n))
    -- if (v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} = v*{v <- v*}[n : nrest])
    -- if (fr = {LOCALS $some_values(v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*}) ++ $default_locals(fi.MODULE, func.LOCALS), MODULE fi.MODULE})
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr [] |- func.BODY : ReturnR s' frp v_result_stack*{v_result_stack <- v_result_stack*}
    -- if (m = |t_result*{t_result <- t_result*}|)
    -- if (v_results*{v_results <- v_results*} = $take_<value>(v_result_stack*{v_result_stack <- v_result_stack*}, m))
    -- if (v'*{v' <- v'*} = v_results*{v_results <- v_results*} ++ v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

  rulegroup ast-break-zero

    rule ast-break-zero: s v*{v <- v*} |- fa : (s', v'*{v' <- v'*})
    -- if (fi = s.FUNCS[fa])
    -- if (AstFunc func = fi.CODE)
    -- if (DefFuncT FuncT t_arg*{t_arg <- t_arg*} t_result*{t_result <- t_result*} = $expanddt(fi.TYPE))
    -- if (n = |t_arg*{t_arg <- t_arg*}|)
    -- if (v_arg_stack*{v_arg_stack <- v_arg_stack*} = $take_<value>(v*{v <- v*}, n))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $rev_<value>(v_arg_stack*{v_arg_stack <- v_arg_stack*}))
    -- if (nrest as int = (|v*{v <- v*}| - n))
    -- if (v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} = v*{v <- v*}[n : nrest])
    -- if (fr = {LOCALS $some_values(v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*}) ++ $default_locals(fi.MODULE, func.LOCALS), MODULE fi.MODULE})
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr [] |- func.BODY : BreakR s' frp 0 v_result_stack*{v_result_stack <- v_result_stack*}
    -- if (m = |t_result*{t_result <- t_result*}|)
    -- if (v_results*{v_results <- v_results*} = $take_<value>(v_result_stack*{v_result_stack <- v_result_stack*}, m))
    -- if (v'*{v' <- v'*} = v_results*{v_results <- v_results*} ++ v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

  rulegroup host-no-result

    rule host-no-result: s v*{v <- v*} |- fa : (s, v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*})
    -- if (fi = s.FUNCS[fa])
    -- if (HostFunc _hostfunc = fi.CODE)
    -- if (DefFuncT FuncT t_arg*{t_arg <- t_arg*} [] = $expanddt(fi.TYPE))
    -- if (|t_arg*{t_arg <- t_arg*}| <= |v*{v <- v*}|)
    -- if (n as int = (|v*{v <- v*}| - |t_arg*{t_arg <- t_arg*}|))
    -- if (v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} = v*{v <- v*}[|t_arg*{t_arg <- t_arg*}| : n])

relation Invoke_funcaddr_throw: store value* |- funcaddr : execresult

  rulegroup ast

    rule ast: s v*{v <- v*} |- fa : ThrowR s' frp ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*}
    -- if (fi = s.FUNCS[fa])
    -- if (AstFunc func = fi.CODE)
    -- if (DefFuncT FuncT t_arg*{t_arg <- t_arg*} t_result*{t_result <- t_result*} = $expanddt(fi.TYPE))
    -- if (n = |t_arg*{t_arg <- t_arg*}|)
    -- if (v_arg_stack*{v_arg_stack <- v_arg_stack*} = $take_<value>(v*{v <- v*}, n))
    -- if (v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} = $rev_<value>(v_arg_stack*{v_arg_stack <- v_arg_stack*}))
    -- if (fr = {LOCALS $some_values(v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*}) ++ $default_locals(fi.MODULE, func.LOCALS), MODULE fi.MODULE})
    -- Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr [] |- func.BODY : ThrowR s' frp ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*}

relation Invoke_ok: store funcaddr value* |- value* : store

  rulegroup 

    rule : s fa v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- v_result*{v_result <- v_result*} : s'
    -- if (v_stack*{v_stack <- v_stack*} = $rev_<value>(v_arg*{v_arg <- v_arg*}))
    -- Invoke_funcaddr: s v_stack*{v_stack <- v_stack*} |- fa : (s', v_stack_result*{v_stack_result <- v_stack_result*})
    -- if (v_result*{v_result <- v_result*} = $rev_<value>(v_stack_result*{v_stack_result <- v_stack_result*}))

relation Handle_throw: store frame value* tagaddr value* |- catch* : execresult

  rulegroup catch

    rule catch: s fr v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*} |- Catch x y :: catch*{catch <- catch*} : BreakR s fr y v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*} ++ v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*}
    -- if (ja = fr.MODULE.TAGS[x])

  rulegroup catch-skip

    rule catch-skip: s fr v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*} |- Catch x y :: catch*{catch <- catch*} : er
    -- if ~(ja = fr.MODULE.TAGS[x])
    -- Handle_throw: s fr v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*} |- catch*{catch <- catch*} : er

  rulegroup catch-ref

    rule catch-ref: s fr v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*} |- CatchRef x y :: catch*{catch <- catch*} : BreakR s' fr y RefV ExnRef a :: v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*} ++ v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*}
    -- if (ja = fr.MODULE.TAGS[x])
    -- if (a = |s.EXNS|)
    -- if (s' = s[EXNS = s.EXNS ++ [{TAG ja, FIELDS v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*}}]])

  rulegroup catch-ref-skip

    rule catch-ref-skip: s fr v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*} |- CatchRef x y :: catch*{catch <- catch*} : er
    -- if ~(ja = fr.MODULE.TAGS[x])
    -- Handle_throw: s fr v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*} |- catch*{catch <- catch*} : er

  rulegroup catch-all

    rule catch-all: s fr v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*} |- CatchAll x :: catch*{catch <- catch*} : BreakR s fr x v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*}

  rulegroup catch-all-ref

    rule catch-all-ref: s fr v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*} |- CatchAllRef x :: catch*{catch <- catch*} : BreakR s' fr x RefV ExnRef a :: v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*}
    -- if (a = |s.EXNS|)
    -- if (s' = s[EXNS = s.EXNS ++ [{TAG ja, FIELDS v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*}}]])

  rulegroup nil

    rule nil: s fr v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*} |- [] : ThrowR s fr ja v_throw*{v_throw <- v_throw*}

def $refs_of_values(value*) : refval* =

  clause 0 : ([]) = []

  clause 1 : (RefV r :: v*{v <- v*}) = r :: $refs_of_values(v*{v <- v*})

def $pack_field(storagetype, value) : fieldval =

  clause 0 : (ValStorageT _valtype, v) = FieldValue v

  clause 1 : (PackStorageT I8, NumV I32 n) = PackedField I8 $byte_of_i32(n)

  clause 2 : (PackStorageT I16, NumV I32 n) = PackedField I16 $i32_of_bytes($bytes_of_i32(n)[0 : 2])

def $pack_fields(fieldtype*, value*) : fieldval* =

  clause 0 : ([], []) = []

  clause 1 : (FieldT mut zt :: yt*{yt <- yt*}, v_1 :: v*{v <- v*}) = $pack_field(zt, v_1) :: $pack_fields(yt*{yt <- yt*}, v*{v <- v*})

def $unpack_field(fieldval) : value =

  clause 0 : (FieldValue v) = v

  clause 1 : (PackedField pt n) = NumV I32 n

def $unpack_field_ext(fieldval, extension) : value =

  clause 0 : (FieldValue v, extension) = v

  clause 1 : (PackedField I8 n, ZX) = NumV I32 n

  clause 2 : (PackedField I16 n, ZX) = NumV I32 n

  clause 3 : (PackedField I8 n, SX) = NumV I32 $i32_extend_i8_s(n)

  clause 4 : (PackedField I16 n, SX) = NumV I32 $i32_extend_i16_s(n)

def $some_values(value*) : value?* =

  clause 0 : ([]) = []

  clause 1 : (v_1 :: v*{v <- v*}) = ?(v_1) :: $some_values(v*{v <- v*})

def $default_locals(moduleinst, local*) : value?* =

  clause 0 : (mm, []) = []

  clause 1 : (mm, {LTYPE t} :: local*{local <- local*}) = $default_value($inst_valtype(mm, t)) :: $default_locals(mm, local*{local <- local*})

def $default_field_values(fieldtype*) : value* =

  clause 0 : ([]) = []

  clause 1 : (FieldT mut zt :: yt*{yt <- yt*}) = v_1 :: $default_field_values(yt*{yt <- yt*})
  -- if (?(v_1) = $default_value($unpacked_storage_type(zt)))

def $storage_size_nat(storagetype) : nat =

  clause 0 : (PackStorageT I8) = 1

  clause 1 : (PackStorageT I16) = 2

  clause 2 : (PackStorageT I32) = 4

  clause 3 : (PackStorageT I64) = 8

  clause 4 : (ValStorageT NumT I32T) = 4

  clause 5 : (ValStorageT NumT I64T) = 8

  clause 6 : (ValStorageT NumT F32T) = 4

  clause 7 : (ValStorageT NumT F64T) = 8

  clause 8 : (ValStorageT VecT V128T) = 16

def $fieldval_of_storage_bytes(storagetype, byte*) : fieldval =

  clause 0 : (PackStorageT I8, b*{b <- b*}) = PackedField I8 b*{b <- b*}[0]

  clause 1 : (PackStorageT I16, b*{b <- b*}) = PackedField I16 $i32_of_bytes(b*{b <- b*})

  clause 2 : (ValStorageT NumT I32T, b*{b <- b*}) = FieldValue NumV I32 $i32_of_bytes(b*{b <- b*})

  clause 3 : (ValStorageT NumT I64T, b*{b <- b*}) = FieldValue NumV I64 $i64_of_bytes(b*{b <- b*}) as i64

def $fieldvals_of_data(storagetype, byte*, nat, nat) : fieldval* =

  clause 0 : (zt, b*{b <- b*}, offset, n) = []
  -- if (n = 0)

  clause 1 : (zt, b*{b <- b*}, offset, n) = fv :: $fieldvals_of_data(zt, b*{b <- b*}, offset', n')
  -- if (n > 0)
  -- if (nsize = $storage_size_nat(zt))
  -- if (fv = $fieldval_of_storage_bytes(zt, b*{b <- b*}[offset : nsize]))
  -- if (offset' = (offset + nsize))
  -- if (n' as int = (n - 1))

def $fieldvals_of_refs(storagetype, refval*) : fieldval* =

  clause 0 : (zt, []) = []

  clause 1 : (zt, r_1 :: r_t*{r_t <- r_t*}) = $pack_field(zt, RefV r_1) :: $fieldvals_of_refs(zt, r_t*{r_t <- r_t*})

def $ref_eq(refval, refval) : nat =

  clause 0 : (NullRef _ht, NullRef _ht') = 1

  clause 1 : (r, r) = 1

  clause -1 : (r_1, r_2) = 0

def $block_result_arity(moduleinst, blocktype) : nat =

  clause 0 : (mm, ValBlockType ?()) = 0

  clause 1 : (mm, ValBlockType ?(t)) = 1

  clause 2 : (mm, VarBlockType x) = |t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*}|
  -- if (DefFuncT FuncT t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*} = $expanddt(mm.TYPES[x]))

def $block_param_arity(moduleinst, blocktype) : nat =

  clause 0 : (mm, ValBlockType ?()) = 0

  clause 1 : (mm, ValBlockType ?(t)) = 0

  clause 2 : (mm, VarBlockType x) = |t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*}|
  -- if (DefFuncT FuncT t_1*{t_1 <- t_1*} t_2*{t_2 <- t_2*} = $expanddt(mm.TYPES[x]))

def $block_input_stack(value*, nat) : value* =

  clause 0 : (v*{v <- v*}, n) = $take_<value>(v*{v <- v*}, n)

def $block_outer_stack(value*, nat) : value* =

  clause 0 : (v*{v <- v*}, n) = v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*}
  -- if (nrest as int = (|v*{v <- v*}| - n))
  -- if (v_rest*{v_rest <- v_rest*} = v*{v <- v*}[n : nrest])

builtin def $bytes_of_text(text) : byte*

def $alloctypes(type*) : deftype* =

  clause 0 : (type*{type <- type*}) = $alloctypes_from([], type*{type <- type*})

def $alloctypes_from(deftype*, type*) : deftype* =

  clause 0 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, []) = dt*{dt <- dt*}

  clause 1 : (dt*{dt <- dt*}, type_1 :: type*{type <- type*}) = $alloctypes_from(dt_all*{dt_all <- dt_all*}, type*{type <- type*})
  -- if (x = |dt*{dt <- dt*}|)
  -- if (dt_rolled*{dt_rolled <- dt_rolled*} = $rolldt(x, type_1))
  -- if (dt_new*{dt_new <- dt_new*} = $subst_deftype<deftype*>(dt*{dt <- dt*}, $subst_of, dt_rolled)*{dt_rolled <- dt_rolled*})
  -- if (dt_all*{dt_all <- dt_all*} = dt*{dt <- dt*} ++ dt_new*{dt_new <- dt_new*})

def $allocexport(moduleinst, export) : exportinst =

  clause 0 : (mm, {NAME name, EDESC FuncExport x}) = {NAME name, ADDR FuncAddr mm.FUNCS[x]}

  clause 1 : (mm, {NAME name, EDESC GlobalExport x}) = {NAME name, ADDR GlobalAddr mm.GLOBALS[x]}

  clause 2 : (mm, {NAME name, EDESC TableExport x}) = {NAME name, ADDR TableAddr mm.TABLES[x]}

  clause 3 : (mm, {NAME name, EDESC MemExport x}) = {NAME name, ADDR MemAddr mm.MEMS[x]}

  clause 4 : (mm, {NAME name, EDESC TagExport x}) = {NAME name, ADDR TagAddr mm.TAGS[x]}

def $allocexports(moduleinst, export*) : exportinst* =

  clause 0 : (mm, export*{export <- export*}) = $allocexport(mm, export)*{export <- export*}

def $import_externtype(moduleinst, import) : externtype =

  clause 0 : (mm, {MODULENAME _name, ITEMNAME _name', IDESC FuncImport x}) = ExtFunct $deftype_at(mm.TYPES, x)

  clause 1 : (mm, {MODULENAME _name, ITEMNAME _name', IDESC GlobalImport gt}) = ExtGlobalT $inst_globaltype(mm, gt)

  clause 2 : (mm, {MODULENAME _name, ITEMNAME _name', IDESC TableImport tt}) = ExtTableT $inst_tabletype(mm, tt)

  clause 3 : (mm, {MODULENAME _name, ITEMNAME _name', IDESC MemImport mt}) = ExtMemoryT $inst_memtype(mm, mt)

  clause 4 : (mm, {MODULENAME _name, ITEMNAME _name', IDESC TagImport x}) = ExtTagT TagT $deftype_at(mm.TYPES, x)
